# Part II — The problem of time, and what the metaphysics may say about it

## §7 The problem, stated at full strength

**7.1 The two equations.** Quantum mechanics evolves a closed system's state by an external, classical, universal parameter that is not an observable:

  iħ ∂ₜ|ψ⟩ = Ĥ|ψ⟩.  (7-1)

Canonical general relativity, written in Hamiltonian (ADM) form, has a Hamiltonian that is a pure constraint: time evolution is a gauge transformation. Quantizing, the Schrödinger equation for a closed universe becomes the Wheeler–DeWitt equation,

  Ĥ Ψ[h, φ] = 0,  (7-2)

in which no time parameter appears at all. This is the frozen formalism (Kuchař 1992; Isham 1993; Anderson 2017; Carlip–Hu 2023 for a modern review). *(Noted 15 October 2026, tenth amendment; §22.13.)* One 2026 preprint contests the step from "no external time" to a stationary global state: a time-reparametrization-invariant evolution law whose solutions are the reparametrized Schrödinger trajectories — the same path in state space, with no speed along it — recovers the Schrödinger predictions relative to an internal clock while the global state is *not* stationary (Oreshkov–Bouvy, arXiv:2608.08065). If it holds, (7-2) is one formulation of the clash rather than its forced form; the plan's reading of time as an output read against an internal clock (§9.4) is unaffected either way. Registered, not adopted.

**7.2 Why it is a genuine clash and not bookkeeping.** Special relativity and quantum mechanics were reconciled by quantum field theory — not by making time an operator but by demoting position to a label, so that both x and t are background parameters on a fixed spacetime. (Pauli's observation that a self-adjoint time operator conjugate to a bounded-below Hamiltonian cannot exist stands; covariant time observables are POVMs, not projection-valued.) Quantum field theory on a fixed curved background also works, with the warning that "particle" becomes slicing-dependent (Unruh 1976). The trouble begins when the metric is dynamical and quantum:

- The *t* of (7-1) is a coordinate; in general relativity coordinates are gauge.
- Under (7-2) the state does not evolve, there is no agreed inner product on its solution space (the equation is Klein–Gordon-like, not Schrödinger-like), and the gauge-invariant observables — those commuting with all constraints — are constants of motion.
- A superposition of geometries is a superposition of causal structures, so there is no definite "before" and "after" in which to pose the only kind of question quantum mechanics knows how to answer: the probability of X at time t.

Time plays three roles in quantum mechanics — the evolution parameter, the "now" on which the state is defined and probabilities normalized, and what clocks read. Relativity pulls them apart: only the third (duration along a worldline) is physical, and the formalism of (7-1) is built on the first two. In the Lighthouse's lexicon (§5): quantum mechanics needs a *label* that is not gauge; relativity has *order* and *duration* and no such label.

**7.3 The families of answer, with status** (this is the field's map, not the plan's verdict).

| Family | Content | Status | What it leaves open |
|---|---|---|---|
| Internal time before quantizing | Pick a physical degree of freedom as clock (scalar or dust field; York's extrinsic-curvature time; unimodular four-volume); solve the constraint for its conjugate momentum; recover a Schrödinger equation | Working method (e.g. loop quantum cosmology with a massless scalar) | Multiple-choice problem (different clocks → inequivalent quantum theories); no clock is global; the clock is itself quantum |
| Semiclassical / emergent time | Born–Oppenheimer on (7-2): heavy gravitational variables define a phase, hence a time; matter obeys Schrödinger with corrections | Established as an approximation scheme (DeWitt; Banks; Halliwell–Hawking; Kiefer) | Validity only where something is classical enough to be a clock; the approximation is not a derivation of time |
| Relational / conditional | Page–Wootters: a stationary global state; evolution as entanglement between clock and rest, read by conditioning; shown equivalent to relational Dirac observables and to deparametrization (Höhn–Smith–Lock 2021) | Theorem-grade in finite and idealizable settings; realized in the laboratory (Moreva et al. 2014) | Which clock; the tensor-product split is not given by the state; constraint algebras with structure functions; ordering vs passage |
| Thermal / modular time | The state determines the flow (Connes–Rovelli 1994): time is the modular flow of the state on the algebra of observables | Conjecture; one exact geometric instance (Bisognano–Wichmann); nontrivial consistency checks (Rovelli 1993; Martinetti–Rovelli 2003); transformed by the crossed-product results of 2022–2025 (§11) | Which state; the semiclassical limit; the Lorentzian signature |
| Covariant | Path integrals over geometries, spin foams, causal dynamical triangulations, general-boundary formulation: time lives on the boundary data | Research programmes | Recovery of a bulk Schrödinger equation; measure on geometries |
| Order fundamental | Causal set theory: causal order and number are primitive; *Order + Number = Geometry*; sequential growth has genuine becoming | Research programme with a clean kinematics | Quantum dynamics; Lorentzian continuum recovery |
| Preferred foliation | Shape dynamics (York time; refoliation invariance traded for spatial conformal invariance); Hořava–Lifshitz | Shape dynamics reproduces GR where a constant-mean-curvature slicing exists; Hořava is tightly constrained by Lorentz-violation bounds | Whether the preferred time is physical or gauge; black-hole interiors; the quantum theory |
| Holography | Bulk time emerges from a boundary theory whose time is the ordinary background parameter | Overwhelming evidence in AdS | Relocates the problem; our universe is not AdS |
| Trajectory (pilot-wave) time *(row added 15 Oct 2026, eighth amendment)* | de Broglie–Bohm quantum cosmology: the Wheeler–DeWitt wavefunctional guides an *actual* 3-geometry and matter configuration; the Schrödinger *t* and the order of events are read off that actual history, so no probability interpretation of Ψ(universe) and no collapse are needed (Vink 1992; Shtanov 1996; Goldstein–Teufel 2001; Pinto-Neto–Fabris 2013) | Working method in minisuperspace (singularity avoidance; perturbation spectra); in full superspace the guidance dynamics is not refoliation-invariant in general — the quantum potential distinguishes a foliation (Shtanov 1996; Pinto-Neto–Fabris 2013) | Whether the distinguished foliation is extra absolute structure or is extracted covariantly from the wavefunction (Dürr–Goldstein–Norsen–Struyve–Zanghì 2014); quantum equilibrium (typicality) for the universe; which variables are beables. This is §16.1 position 4 in its cosmological form |
| Modify QM | Time-of-arrival POVMs; parametrized relativistic QM; gravity-induced collapse (Diósi–Penrose) — Penrose's argument *is* the problem of time made physical: two superposed mass distributions are two spacetimes whose time translations cannot be identified | Collapse models live and tightening; the parameter-free Diósi–Penrose model is excluded (Donadi et al. 2021) and the remaining space is cut further by XENONnT (PRL 136 (2026) 120201) | The remaining parameter space; D-7 bets against the family |

**7.4 Where the field stands.** If there is a majority view among quantum-gravity theorists, it is that the absolute time of quantum mechanics is not fundamental: at the deepest level the description is timeless or relational, and the Schrödinger *t* is what one obtains when some degree of freedom is classical enough to serve as a clock. The disagreement is over everything that matters: which variables are clocks; whether the emergence is exact or approximate; whether a preferred foliation is secretly required; and whether the *flow* and *arrow* of time need ingredients beyond the existence of a time variable. The *arrow* is a separate problem (§17). The problem of time is whether, and how, a time variable exists at all.

**7.5 The experimental interface, so far.** No experiment discriminates between the families. The interface is becoming testable at its edges: optical clocks resolving gravitational redshift across a millimetre (2022); proposals to place a clock in a superposition of heights (Zych et al.); tabletop gravitationally-induced-entanglement proposals (Bose et al.; Marletto–Vedral); quantum-switch realizations of indefinite causal order (optical, not gravitational). The plan's WP7 is pointed at this interface. *Status, 15 October 2026 (tenth amendment; §22.13):* no gravitationally-induced-entanglement experiment has been performed; the interpretive dispute over what such an experiment would show has sharpened rather than closed — a Newton–Cartan argument that a classical mediating gravity cannot supply the entanglement (Schneider–Huggett–Linnemann 2025, preprint), a claim that single-mass interferometry already verifies gravity-mediated entanglement *indirectly* under one of two assumptions (Plávala, PRD 113 (2026) 085004; contested), and analyses of the subsystem-independence and shielding assumptions (Boulle–Franzmann, PRD 114 (2026) 025015; Bulling et al. 2026). The plan counts none of these as the discriminating experiment; clocks in superposition remain proposals (Paczos–Foo–Zych, Quantum 9 (2025) 1827).

## §8 What the metaphysics may say — the override applied

The v2.0 plan adopted (its §6) a non-dual working assumption and used it, after retracting its attempted derivations, as a *selection filter* on fundamental theories. The Lighthouse replaces the assumption. The table records what the filter said under each metaphysics; the right-hand column governs.

| Item (v2.0) | Under the non-dual assumption | Under the Lighthouse (real distinction) |
|---|---|---|
| *No privileged subsystem or clock* | Required: excluded preferred foliations; rejected LQG ("privileged kinematical graph"), CDT's foliation time, causal sets as ontology | **Not required.** Creation has real levels; a privileged state or clock is admissible. The verdicts of v2.0 §8.4 become criterion-relative (§13), and the time fork is a live prediction (D-14) |
| *Unique state up to gauge* (from "necessity") | Selected | **Dropped.** The created state is contingent. State selection (§11.6) is the target, and D-8 requires the selection to be lawful rather than brute |
| *Self-existence* = modular fixed point ΔΩ = Ω; "the mind has nothing but itself to know itself with" | Image of the Absolute | **Struck** (§6, items 4 and 9). The fixed point is a theorem about any cyclic separating vector and images nothing |
| *The standard form is the Absolute* | Interpretation | **Forbidden** (§1: the ground is not pictured). The standard form is mathematics of the created order's observables |
| *Structural circularity*: one-way derivation ill-posed; dissolution "unconditionally available" | Mandatory consequence of non-duality | **Deleted** (§6, items 16 and 18). Grounding is one-way and outside physics; emergence is one-way inside it. The plan has derivation and mechanism tracks only |
| *No observer in the structure; apparent observers are conditioned substructures* (v2.0 §9.3) | — | Observers are real agents. The formalism of §9 is unchanged; the gloss is |
| *Type III₁ as the non-dual algebra* — "the deepest resonance" | Load-bearing resonance | At most axis evidence (§6, item 6) — and, per §11, the resonance was with an algebra that lacked its clock |
| *Timelessness* → time derived from state | Selected | **Kept, re-grounded:** the ground is prior in being, not in time; time belongs to the created order; whether the fundamental physical description is stationary is a question for physics (it is, in the canonical setting, by (7-2); one 2026 preprint contests the step, §7.1). *The text's own idiom (tenth amendment; §5 "Time, in the text"):* creature-duration is counted against created cycles (10:5; 17:12), and "a day with your Lord" is explicitly not "of what you count" (22:47) — the relativity of *reckoning*, and no more than that |
| *Spacelessness* → space derived | Selected | **Open** (P9). The Lighthouse does not require space to be emergent |
| *Simplicity* → factor algebra (trivial centre) | Selected | **Not inferable.** Divine simplicity is about God, not about the centre of the algebra of created observables. Whether the gravitational algebra is a factor is a physics question — and the crossed-product constructions give answers that depend on the setting (§11) |
| Consciousness glues (modular ↔ Φ; modular ↔ neural correlates) and the qualia placeholder | In the architecture, excluded from the core | **Struck** from the architecture; the licensed near-side questions are N-3 and N-7 (§19) |

**What the override gives the plan that non-duality could not.** Three things. (i) *Contingency:* the Past Hypothesis and any state-selection principle were an embarrassment to a necessary Absolute; under creation they are what one expects, and the demand on them shifts from "why any selection?" to "is the selection lawful?" (D-8). (ii) *Permission for a privileged clock:* the single most consequential change, because the measure-first heuristic's sharpest physical forms (§12) are preferred-time forms. (iii) *Real levels:* the plan's layered architecture (§14) is no longer "appearances of the one" but a hierarchy of real, grounded structures, which is what a dependency architecture needs in order to be about anything.

**One cost the override adds.** v2.0 §9.3 read quantum "actualization" relationally: definiteness relative to the correlated subsystem, not absolute (relational quantum mechanics). The Lighthouse's measurement reading says the definite outcome is the **I2**-intrinsic fact. Override wins: facts are absolute — known to God, perspectival only for creatures. That commitment has a price Bell alone does not charge: the extended Wigner's-friend theorems (Frauchiger–Renner 2018; Bong et al. 2020, "local friendliness") show that *absoluteness of observed events* + locality + no-superdeterminism is inconsistent with quantum predictions. So D-7 plus absoluteness forces nonlocal *actualization*, not merely nonlocal correlation. The Lighthouse already holds locality to be emergent, so the position is consistent; the cost is entered in the ledger (§16; G20).

**What the metaphysics may not do, restated for this problem.** It may not supply a clock (God is not a clock, a field, or a frame). It may not fill the gap where the inner product on solutions of (7-2) is missing. It may not read a mechanism of emergence off any verse — nor time dilation, or any ratio of durations, out of 22:47, 32:5 or 70:4 (§5, "Time, in the text"). It may not call the problem dissolved. What it may do is what it has done in this section: fix the jurisdiction, remove a false constraint (no privileged clock), add a true permission (contingency), and insist that every remaining question be answered by construction or left open.

# Part III — The plan

## §9 The relational core

**9.1 The mechanism** (Page–Wootters 1983). A universe satisfying a constraint of the form (7-2) is partitioned into a clock C and a system S with non-interacting total Hamiltonian Ĥ = Ĥ_C ⊗ 1 + 1 ⊗ Ĥ_S. The clock carries a time observable T̂_C conjugate to Ĥ_C (ideally [T̂_C, Ĥ_C] = iħ; realistically a covariant POVM), with clock states |t⟩. Expanding the global state in the clock basis, |Ψ⟩⟩ = ∫dt |t⟩ ⊗ |ψ(t)⟩, the constraint yields the conditional Schrödinger equation iħ ∂ₜ|ψ(t)⟩ = Ĥ_S|ψ(t)⟩. "At time t" means "conditioned on the clock reading t." The correlations are entanglement in a stationary global state; an internal observer sees ordinary evolution; the global state does not evolve. Realized with an entangled photon pair, one photon serving as the clock for the other (Moreva et al. 2014).

**9.2 Critique and repair.** Kuchař's objection — that conditioning applies a projection that does not commute with the constraint, and so yields wrong propagators for relativistic systems — is answered, within its scope, by the *Trinity* (Höhn–Smith–Lock 2021), which establishes by explicit reduction maps the equivalence of three pictures: relational Dirac observables in a clock-neutral formulation; Page–Wootters conditional states; and a relational Heisenberg picture obtained by symmetry reduction. Relational observables are quantized with covariant POVMs encompassing non-ideal clocks, and conditional probabilities are computed in the physical Hilbert space from the start. Giovannetti–Lloyd–Maccone (2015) had already repaired the propagator issue by conditioning on clock POVMs. Castro-Ruiz, Giacomini, Belenchia and Brukner (2020) extend the picture to clocks that gravitate and to the temporal localizability of events among them.

**Scope [C].** The constructions are theorem-grade in finite-dimensional and idealizable settings with constant constraint algebras. Gravity has a constraint algebra with structure functions; the observable technology is not directly available there (G6). The first work package is confined to constant algebras.

**9.3 What remains open, and where each item goes.**

1. **The clock ambiguity (multiple-choice problem).** Different clock choices produce inequivalent conditional dynamics; nothing in the state ranks them. In v2.0 this was "the central quantitative object." In v2.1 it is sharpened twice: algebraically, clock choice is state choice and the transformation between clocks is the Connes cocycle (§11.3); and in the gravitational algebras of §11.4 the choice is *not* pure gauge — different clocks give different algebras and different entropies (De Vuyst–Eccles–Höhn–Kirklin 2024–25). A 2026 preprint sharpens it a third time, in the plan's direction: under a purely relational stance the ambiguity extends from histories to the *evolution laws* themselves (an ideal clock's spectrum uniformizes the world's evolution operators, leaving only Hilbert-space dimension invariant), the non-interaction condition does not remove it, and what removes it is the physical meaning of the operators — something the bare Page–Wootters structure does not contain (Stoica 2026, arXiv:2604.21805; preprint, §22.10). A second preprint by the same author makes the point from the state side: an entangled clock–world vector has infinitely many product decompositions, each a valid time operator of the *same* clock, each giving a different history, so the reading on which the world is passively conditioned on a clock reading cannot hold — fixing "the" time operator imports the temporal meaning Page–Wootters was to explain (Stoica 2026b, arXiv:2608.16732; preprint, §22.13). The open question is P15.
2. **The tensor-product structure.** Page–Wootters presupposes a split into clock and rest. The Wheeler–DeWitt state does not come with one. Any principle that selects the split from the Hamiltonian's spectrum alone (the "quantum mereology" programme of Carroll–Singh 2021 is one attempt) is a principle that derives a clock from a measure in sense **M2** — and is, for that reason, exactly what the Lighthouse's heuristic asks for. Registered as G17. (Stoica 2026 adds a constraint on the candidates: fixing the split by fiat removes the ambiguity but "blocks spacetime symmetries", and treating all splits as equally valid perspectives makes records uncorrelated with what they record; the selecting principle must do neither.) Theorem 10.2 adds a constraint on any answer: once a clock system is fixed, "the rest given the clock" is not a system of the normalized theory, so the split cannot be undone inside the theory by currying; conditional dynamics is a higher-order object, which is the type the Trinity's relational observables already have. *Added 15 October 2026 (tenth amendment; §22.13; §23.2 item 99).* The split now has a non-perturbative cousin. Gravitational-path-integral and quantum-extremal-surface arguments give a **one-dimensional, real Hilbert space for a closed universe without an observer**; including an observer *Ob* — a subsystem with many degrees of freedom in a pointer basis, nothing more — yields a quantum mechanics on a Hilbert space of dimension ≈ e^{S_Ob}, with errors exponentially small in S_Ob (Harlow–Usatyuk–Zhao, JHEP 02 (2026) 108), or of dimension scaling as e^{1/G_N} under a different rule for keeping the observer in its universe (Abdalla–Antonini–Iliesiu–Levine, JHEP 05 (2025) 059); the two prescriptions differ and give different dimensions (Akers et al., JHEP 05 (2025) 201). For this plan the result is the strongest available form of the thesis that time is the output of a split: with no split there is not merely no evolution — there is no state space in which anything could happen; and the dependence of the *dimension* on how the observer is included is dead end 8 ("the clock ambiguity as pure gauge") refuted at a deeper level than DEHK's. Graded **[H]** (path-integral evidence; prescriptions not yet unique). Nothing in it is about minds (§6, item 8), and nothing in it supplies the selecting principle G17 asks for.
3. **Ideal clocks.** Finite precision, back-reaction, decoherence; corrections only partially understood (G2; WP1). *Sharpened (tenth amendment):* the idealization fails first in the **measurement model** — the twirled-observable and purified-measurement implementations of a Page–Wootters measurement agree for ideal clocks and describe operationally distinct situations for finite-resource clocks (Hausmann–Schmidhuber–Castro-Ruiz, Quantum 9 (2025) 1616); and a single composite system serving as both rod and clock obeys a Heisenberg-like trade-off between spatial and temporal resolution, from quantum speed limits plus mass–energy equivalence (Mattei–Castro-Ruiz 2025, preprint) — a conjugate pair in §4's sense, and a resource bound for WP1 and WP6.
4. **Dynamical geometry.** The mechanism is demonstrated on fixed backgrounds (G6; the crossed-product constructions of §11.4 are the first setting in which the clock is inserted *with* the gravitational constraint).
5. **Ordering versus passage.** Conditional correlations give a B-series (an ordering), not an A-series (a flow). "Becoming" is a posit the formalism does not supply. The one programme that supplies it — causal-set sequential growth — is admitted to the landscape at §13. The question is excluded from the falsifiable core (v2.0 §12.3 retained).

**9.4 Time as output — the ledger** *(added 15 October 2026, sixth amendment; prompted by clue 9, a transcript asking "time is input — what would be an account of time as output?"; §23.2, items 78–82; review `v2.2/assessments/time-as-output-review.md`; checks `v2.2/verify/clock_checks.py`, 12/12).* "Time" is not one thing to be output; it is at least four, and this plan's results already say, for each, what is output of what. The ledger collects them.

| Component | Account of it as *output* | Of what? | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Order** (before/after; a B-series) | Conditional correlations with a clock subsystem (§9.1) | The constraint plus a **tensor split** into clock and rest | Derived *given* the split; the split is not derived (§9.3 item 2; G17; P15). Causal sets take order as primitive instead (§13). |
| **Flow** (a one-parameter group; the *t* of dynamics) | The modular flow σ_t^ω of (algebra, state); the Connes cocycle makes the *outer* flow canonical for a type III₁ factor (§11.2–11.3); the crossed product inserts a clock with the constraint respected (§11.4) | The **algebra and a state** — no time put in | Theorem-level in the algebra; identification with physical time is the thermal-time hypothesis [H] (G4; D-14 Branch B). Parametrized, **not oriented**: σ_t and σ_{−t} are on a par. |
| **Duration** (how much) | A scale carried by the state — the KMS inverse temperature — or a volume/York variable (§12) | The **state/measure** | "Measure precedes duration" (§4.2, commitment (i); Conjecture M); not obtainable from the flow alone. |
| **Arrow** (orientation) | None from the formalism: no modular ground state (Prop. 11.1); the generalized second law is the arrow *in form*, the low-entropy condition the arrow *in fact*, contingent (§17.1–17.2; A9; D-8) | A **contingent state** | Never output of structure; the orientation problem (§17.1(b)) remains in addition; "becoming" is a posit (§9.3 item 5). |
| **Observer-level time** (ticks, timestamps, an emitted event order) | A bounded generator (clue 8's commitment regime; clue 9's "clock theory") | The generator, **relative to its own step sequence** (a meta-time) | Correct and ordinary — every clock, computer and organism does this; it leaves the physical problem untouched, because the meta-time is what is to be accounted for. |
| **Trajectory time** *(row added 15 Oct 2026, eighth amendment; clue 11)* | Order and duration read off an actual configuration history guided by the wavefunction — de Broglie–Bohm quantum cosmology (§7.3) | The wavefunction **plus an actual configuration** (a beable) and a guidance law | Derived given the beable; the guidance law distinguishes a foliation in general, which is the price (§7.3); the arrow is *not* supplied — the dynamics is time-reversal symmetric and the arrow needs quantum equilibrium plus a low-entropy state, Prop. 11.1's pattern again. This is what §16.1 position 4 does to time. |

**Statement.** An account of time as output with *no* meta-time is exactly the modular programme of §11, and the ledger records what it yields: flow, yes — canonically up to inner automorphisms; order, yes given a split; duration, given a state; arrow, never. This is the plan's position restated from the side of the question, and it is why Rule 7 reads as it does: a one-way derivation of some components of time from a stationary structure is ordinary emergence and physics' business; the dependence of the created order, time included, on its ground is not a derivation and is never in physics' hands.

*A lemma from the "clock theory" (verified).* In the deterministic (commitment) regime the L-tick clock (1 0^{L−1})^∞ has state complexity exactly L, and its commitment gap is Com(M) = 1/L for every M < L and 0 at M = L: below the threshold no machine does better than no clock at all (proof by the Chinese remainder theorem on the common period; exhaustive check for L = 3…6). The deterministic clock is all-or-nothing; only a *stochastic* clock can be partially right (the graded gap curve of §10.6 addendum, item 74).

**In plain terms.** Split the universe into a clock and everything else, and ask what everything else is doing given the clock's reading: the answer obeys ordinary quantum mechanics, and a photon experiment has shown it. The old objection has a careful repair, in small systems. The residue is the choice itself — which part is the clock, and which split of the whole into parts — and this plan treats that residue not as a nuisance but as the thing to be derived.

## §10 The formal no-go layer, corrected

v2.0 unified its two analogies — a chess game read only through its notation, and a sphere whose inhabitants cannot reach the ambient room — into a "temporal confinement triple" and a conjecture (C-NOADJ) that it called the formal core's load-bearing wall. The analogies are kept in formalized form. The conjecture, as stated, is false, and the correction changes what the wall is.

**10.1 The analogies, formalized** (kept from v2.0 §4). *Chess:* E a finite set of half-moves totally ordered by ⪯; the move number c₀(eᵢ) = i; the players' time τ: E → ℝ strictly increasing with increments Δᵢ (thinking times). The intrinsic description is (E, ⪯); the external description is (E, ⪯, τ); the two clocks are related by a monotone, generally nonlinear change of variable, and an observer with access only to c₀ cannot reconstruct τ because the lost information is exactly the increment vector. Two corrections stand: the chess order is total and general relativity's is partial (Layer 1 uses causal sets); and chess has a preferred clock by rule, general relativity does not. The "twin paradox" reading of two chess clocks was refuted in v2.0 and stays refuted. *Sphere:* (M, g) isometrically embedded by i: M ↪ (E, h); intrinsic distance (arc) versus extrinsic distance (chord); the chord is not a path in the inhabitants' world. The mandatory repair (G3) stands: general relativity has no ambient room, so i is reinterpreted as the inclusion of the physical (constraint-satisfying) sector into the kinematical arena — the two-tier structure of Dirac quantization — and τ lives on the auxiliary space and is never an observable.

**10.2 The triple and the functor** (v2.0 §5.1, restated). Σ a causal poset or Lorentzian manifold with its intrinsic order; i: Σ → M the inclusion into the auxiliary kinematical space; τ: M → ℝ a global parameter on the auxiliary space. The category **Int** has ordered structures (X, ⪯) and order-preserving maps; **Ext** has parameter-equipped ordered structures (X, ⪯, τ) with τ monotone, and order- and parameter-preserving maps (f with τ′∘f = τ). The forgetful functor U: **Ext** → **Int** discards τ. A *clock* is a section of U. v2.0 conjectured that U has no natural adjoint, and read that absence as "no universal clock."

**10.3 The correction (theorem).** **Ext** is the slice category **Int**/(ℝ, ≤): ordered structures over the real line. For any category with binary products, the forgetful functor from a slice C/B to C has a right adjoint, X ↦ (X × B → B). Posets have products (the product order). Hence:

> **Proposition 10.1.** U: **Int**/ℝ → **Int** has a right adjoint R: X ↦ (X × ℝ, product order, τ = projection to ℝ). The unit sends (Y, σ) to y ↦ (y, σ(y)); the bijection **Ext**((Y, σ), R X) ≅ **Int**(Y, X) is f ↦ π₁∘f. U has no left adjoint: a left adjoint L would make **Ext**(L X, (ℝ, id)) — a one-element set — biject with **Int**(X, ℝ), which has more than one element whenever X is non-empty.

So an adjoint exists, canonically, and it is worth saying what it is: R X is X with an independent real line adjoined at every event — **Newtonian absolute time**, the cofree clock. What is genuinely absent is a natural *section* that selects one clock correlated with the order. Even that fails for the wrong reason: with τ merely monotone, the constant clock τ ≡ 0 is a natural section; with τ required strictly monotone, no section exists at all because some posets (ω₁, for instance) admit no strictly monotone real-valued map — a cardinality fact, not a physical one. The poset formalization therefore carries none of the intended content. It is retained only as a definition of vocabulary (dead end 15).

**10.4 Where the content actually lives — now verified.** v2.0's evidence for "no universal clock" came from an unrefereed manuscript on quantum combs (v2.0 ref. [10]), described second-hand. The manuscript itself, in its eighth revision (Abaee, *The Opfibration Ontology: Quantum Instruments, Irreversibility, and the Epistemic Asymptote*, Rev. 2.8, October 2026), was supplied after v2.1 was issued. Its main-text theorems were re-derived independently for this document — twenty symbolic and numerical checks, all passing (`v2.2/verify/opfibration_checks.py`, log alongside). What it proves, in its own registers:

> **Theorem 10.2 (no adjoint to environment decoration in normalized process categories; Rev. 2.8 Thms 4.3, 4.5, 4.8, 4.12, 5.2, Prop. 5.1, Thm C.2).** Let E be a finite-dimensional quantum system with d_E ≥ 2. (i) In **Chan** (completely positive trace-preserving maps between finite-dimensional systems) the functor −⊗E has no right adjoint: a right adjoint would give an affine bijection Chan(E,E) ≅ States(R(E)), forcing d_R² = d_E⁴ − d_E² + 1, which lies strictly between the consecutive squares (d_E²−1)² and d_E⁴. (ii) More strongly, for d_E, d_B ≥ 2 the convex body Chan(E,B) is not affinely isomorphic to the normal state space of any Hilbert space, finite or infinite: its extreme boundary has real dimension 2d_E²(d_B−1), the pure states of a d_R-dimensional system form a manifold of dimension 2(d_R−1), and the two dimension counts cannot be matched simultaneously. (iii) In the category of finite-outcome instruments (ordered tuples, or their outcome-permutation quotient) the presheaf A ↦ Instr(A⊗E, B) is not representable for *any* target B: comparing the one- and two-outcome slices at A = ℂ, the representing side gives q(2) − 2q(1) = 1 and the process side gives d_E². (iv) The same two-slice mismatch holds for classical finite-outcome stochastic instruments: the obstruction is normalization, not quantumness. (v) −⊗E has no left adjoint in Chan or in Instr; Instr has neither terminal nor initial object. (vi) In unnormalized **CPM** the right adjoint exists and is E*⊗−.

*Register:* theorem — elementary, and independently re-derived here for (i)–(vi) in their main-text forms. The appendix's extensions (coarse-grained, zero-dropping, dyadic and measurable-outcome quotients; infinite-dimensional E with a separable program register, C.5–C.28; the programming benchmarks C.29–C.35) were not re-derived and are carried as the author's, unrefereed. The headline of (i) agrees with folklore in categorical quantum mechanics — CPTP is semicartesian (discarding is unique), not closed, which is why Kissinger and Uijlen built **Caus** — and the manuscript makes no priority claim. The manuscript's own value is the quantified, pointwise and quotient-by-quotient treatment, and something rarer: the discipline of its retractions.

**[R7 adjudication, 14 October 2026.]** The manuscript's open question R7 (§7.2) — whether, on the non-degenerate locus of Prop. 4.7 where the square-count of Theorem 4.5 is silent (e.g. (d_E,d_B,n,j,d_G) = (7,1,2,2,5), (11,1,3,2,9), (4,2,1,1,7); 49 cells with d_E,d_B ≤ 60), a fixed-grade A-natural affine bijection Instr_n(A⊗E, A⊗B) ≅ Chan(A, G) could still exist — was tested in this session (`v2.2/verify/r7_check.py`, 15/15 checks) after a user-supplied probe-and-reply exchange. Result: R7 is *closed in the narrowed form*. At A = ℂ the right-hand side is the state space of G, whose extreme boundary is CP^{d_G−1}, of real dimension Y = 2(d_G−1); the left-hand side contains, by zero-padding, the extreme channels Chan(E,B) (dimension 2d_E²(d_B−1), Theorem C.2) or, for d_B = 1, the projective binary measurements Gr(r, d_E); so its extreme boundary has dimension X ≥ 2d_E²(d_B−1) for d_B ≥ 2 and X ≥ 2⌊d_E/2⌋⌈d_E/2⌉ for d_B = 1, and X − Y = 2d_E(d_B−2)(d_E−1) + 2d_E(d_E−2) + 4 ≥ 4 in the first case and X − Y ≥ 2⌊d_E²/4⌋ − 2(d_E−3) > 0 in the second (there j = 1 is excluded arithmetically, since it would need (2d_E−1) | 3, so d_G ≤ d_E−2). An affine bijection is a homeomorphism carrying extreme points to extreme points, so none exists at A = ℂ, hence no A-natural family and no fixed-grade representation on any non-degenerate cell. Two corrections to the exchange itself: the probe's exact transport of Theorem C.2 to n-outcome instruments is false as an equality (the extreme set of three-outcome measurements on ℂ¹¹ contains a certified stratum of three rank-4 effects of dimension 95, and one of ranks (5,5,6) of dimension 145, against the probe's 80; the qubit trine stratum has dimension 5, not 2), and its d_B = 1 certificate (d_E−2)(d_E−3)+2 overstates the gap by a factor of two (on (7,1,2,2,5) the gap is exactly 16, not 22); both affect magnitudes, not the sign. Not established, and still open: that the two bodies are non-isomorphic *for every* A — for d_A ≥ 2 both sides carry rank-split strata and must be compared directly — and everything in R1–R6, R8.

**What the manuscript itself withdraws.** Rev. 2.8 removes, in its own words, every reading v2.0 took from the earlier draft. "Adjointness is not a signature of irreversibility … a fortiori it cannot be a signature of time direction" (§5.1). The base of the "causal opfibration" is "the extension relation among stages, not a temporal order; the original Past/Future labels have no model here" (Rem. 4.16). "What does not follow: irreversibility of dynamics, an arrow of time, apparatus-dependence of retrodiction, or non-existence of exact recovery" (§5.5). The earlier draft's "epistemic asymptote" is replaced by a scope section (§7.3). And the "intercept" d_A²(d_E²−1) is identified as an affine codimension — exactly the codimension of the typed channel slice inside the state space of E*⊗B (C.17) — and is explicitly *not* an operational cost or a distortion (open problem R8). So v2.0's "intercept principle" as a wall against intrinsic observers was never the manuscript's claim in its mature form. Proposition 10.1 and Theorem 10.2 now say the same thing from two sides: *the obstruction is normalization, and normalization is not time.*

**Theorem C-NOADJ (v2.2; replaces Conjecture C-NOADJ).** *In the finite-dimensional normalized process categories — Chan, finite-outcome instruments and their permutation quotient, and classical finite-outcome instruments — decoration −⊗E by a nontrivial system E has no adjoint on either side; the would-be internal hom [E, B] is not a system of the theory but a higher-order (comb-type) convex object.* Register: theorem (Theorem 10.2). **Conjecture C-NOADJ′ (what remains open).** *The same holds for normal channels between von Neumann algebras of types II and III, and for the clock-dressed gravitational algebras of §11.4.* Register: conjecture. The dimension counts do not transfer (the hom-sets are infinite-dimensional); the manuscript's C.5 — a continuum of extreme channels whose exact programs must be mutually orthogonal — is the method that might, and it currently needs a separable register. v2.0's "five-fold equivalence" and its clause "no universal finite-memory evaluator recovers a clock-free description" are *not* restored: the manuscript proves non-representability, not an epistemic limit; its no-programming theorem (C.5) is the Nielsen–Chuang obstruction in a new form and says nothing about observers.

**The reading for the time problem (interpretation, kept small).** Let the clock be a system E of a normalized process theory. Theorem 10.2 says that "the rest, conditioned on the clock" is not a system of that theory: conditional dynamics is a higher-order object — an element of the internal hom that exists only in a completion. That is a categorical reason for a choice the Trinity already made: relational content is carried by relational *observables* (maps) and frame changes (supermaps), not by a conditional *system*. Nothing about the direction, rate or selection of time follows, and the theorem holds for classical, quantum, reversible and irreversible dynamics alike.

**10.5 The layer distinction** (v2.0 Table 5-1, kept; statuses updated). First-order normalized categories (**Chan**, **Instr**, **FinStoch**): no internal hom for −⊗E (Theorem 10.2). Higher-order compact-closed (**Caus[CPM]**): internal homs exist; price: higher-order types, trace preservation lost at the base. Unnormalized (**CPM**): internal homs exist; price: probability conservation lost. The reformulation that follows is one of v2.0's cleanest surviving statements and is kept: *the external time of quantum mechanics is a higher-order causal structure — not representable in the first-order normalized process theory, emerging as an internal hom at the price of a higher-order type system.* In the analogy, the first-order category is the sphere's surface and **Caus[CPM]** is the room. In the Lighthouse's grammar (§4) this is an adjunction-type axis, and Proposition 10.1 adds a second, humbler one: the slice adjunction whose right adjoint is Newtonian time. Of the three questions v2.1 sent to WP3: the first — whether the first-order category sits inside a higher-order completion under a physically motivated condition — is now partly answered by the manuscript itself, which constructs a typed affine-slice completion 𝒯 in which −⊗𝔈 ⊣ [𝔈, −] for every finite-dimensional typed input, whose first-order subcategory is Chan and whose Bell counit is trace-preserving only on the admissible slice (C.15–C.19), and proves closedness for the balanced subcategory of **Caus[CPM(FHilb)]** (C.24), with maximality and equivalence beyond it open (R5). The second — whether the intercept is a cohomological obstruction — is dropped: the intercept is an affine codimension (C.17), and no class has been exhibited. The third — whether the Connes cocycle of §11.3 supplies descent data between the layers — is the one genuinely open question and moves to WP5.

**10.6 The rate–distortion shell** (v2.0 §5.4; status changed: decoupled). With T_ext the auxiliary parameter and C a clock reading, R(D) = min I(T_ext; C) subject to mean-squared estimation error ≤ D prices each clock. This is Shannon theory and stands on its own; it neither supports nor is supported by C-NOADJ — Rev. 2.8 §5.4 and R8 state that δ_n = d_E²−1 is not a cost or distortion measure, and v2.0's phrase "supports C-NOADJ" is withdrawn. Status: a formal shell with one concrete candidate functional, the entropy difference between clock choices in the gravitational algebras of §11.4 (G4; WP2).

*Addendum (fifth amendment, 15 October 2026 — §23.2, items 73–74).* The shell's intuition now has a worked-out general theory by the same author as Rev. 2.8: *The Rate–Distortion Theory of Bounded Sequential Transduction* (Zenodo, 10.5281/zenodo.22260638, 2 September 2026; audited in `v2.2/assessments/bst-rate-distortion-review.md`, independent checks `v2.2/verify/bst_checks.py` 17/17, the author's two programs re-run with identical output). Three things follow for this section, none of them a change of status. **(i) Different rate.** There the rate is a *state budget* M (the index of a right congruence on histories, or an operator rank), not an information rate in bits; there is no coding theorem; the relation to the R(D) above is analogy. **(ii) Time is presupposed.** Every object is defined over prefix-ordered histories and causal functions I^ℕ → O^ℕ; the "temporal axis" is the round index of an online protocol; the retention regime is the causal-state construction of computational mechanics, oriented past → future by convention. The theory is a theory of bounded modelling *inside* a given order — a fourth independent instance of the pattern behind Prop. 11.1: where a formalism contains a direction, the direction entered as a hypothesis. The paper claims nothing else (a scan finds no physics claim of any kind). **(iii) The asymptote, corrected from the right side.** What v2.0 called an "epistemic asymptote" and Rev. 2.8 withdrew (item 17) is, done properly, a *gap curve* Δ(M) with **zero thresholds at finite budget** — κ_det(F) for exact commitment, the refinement index N* for retention (which exceeds the number of distinct predictive kernels when lumpability binds), rank H_ν for linear grounding — and a true asymptote only for infinite-rank targets, where the unrestricted shortfall is exactly σ_{M+1}(H_ν) (Eckart–Young–Mirsky) and the Hankel-structured shortfall is ≥ σ_{M+1}, with equality in the one-letter Adamjan–Arov–Krein case and open in the multiletter case. A bounded observer of a finite-state world is exact above a finite threshold; of an infinite-rank world, short by a computable number. Neither is a wall against intrinsic observers. Item 17 stays withdrawn; the shell keeps its status (decoupled; G4; WP2) and gains the correct formal object to point at. The one place where the apparatus touches the arrow — direction-dependent retention budgets, via causal irreversibility C_μ⁺ ≠ C_μ⁻ (Crutchfield–Ellison–Mahoney 2009) — is stated as an open problem in the review (§4(d)) and logged as item 76: a *diagnostic* of time-asymmetry in a law, never a source of it.

**10.7 Irreversibility and reversal, as the manuscript leaves them** (new). Two results of Rev. 2.8 §6 are relevant beyond the no-go layer and both were checked numerically. *Exact recovery is a property of the channel* (Prop. 6.1): a channel has a CPTP left inverse iff its Kraus operators satisfy the full-space Knill–Laflamme condition K_i†K_j = c_ij I, i.e. iff it is a flagged mixture of isometries with orthogonal ranges — more than a single isometry, which the earlier draft had claimed. *Reversal is prior-indexed* (Prop. 6.2): for a full-rank prior σ and τ = N(σ) > 0, the Petz transpose N^♯_σ(X) = σ^{1/2} N*(τ^{−1/2} X τ^{−1/2}) σ^{1/2} is CPTP, recovers σ from τ, and composes contravariantly, (M∘N)^♯_σ = N^♯_σ ∘ M^♯_τ — a functor on *pointed* channels, not on channels. In the language of §11 this is the same lesson from the other side: the Petz map for faithful normal states on a von Neumann algebra is built from relative modular operators (Petz 1988), so **the state that fixes the flow is the state that fixes the reversal, and the category fixes neither** (dead end 5). Register: theorem for each half; the juxtaposition is an interpretation that §17 uses. Finally, the one categorical asymmetry that survives — ℂ is terminal but not initial in Chan; "discarding is unique, preparation is not" (Prop. 5.5; the Coecke–Lal causality axiom) — is *order* in the lexicon of §5: presupposed by a process theory, not derived by it, and not an arrow.

**In plain terms.** Forget everything about a world except which events come before which; a clock is any principled way of putting a parameter back. v2.0 said there is no canonical way to do this. There is: adjoin a free real line to every event — Newton's absolute time, exactly what relativity denies. The real obstruction is elsewhere, and it is now a theorem rather than a report: in any theory that must conserve probability, you cannot fold a nontrivial clock into the system it reads and get back a system of the same theory — "the rest, given the clock" is a different kind of object, one order up. The author of the manuscript that first proposed this has since removed every claim that it says anything about the direction of time, the irreversibility of processes, or the limits of observers; the mathematics was checked here and holds. There is also a door: in a richer, higher-order theory of processes the missing construction exists, and the manuscript now builds one.

## §11 The modular programme, re-anchored and brought up to date

v2.0's fourth arc assembled the modular theory of von Neumann algebras as the language of a state that defines its own time flow, then retracted every derivation of it from metaphysical attributes while keeping the mathematics. v2.1 keeps the mathematics, states it without the glosses the Lighthouse forbids, corrects two statements, and adds the 2022–2025 results in which the programme's central move — put the clock inside the algebra — has been carried out with the gravitational constraint.

**11.1 The pair, and the fixed point (theorem).** The fundamental object is a von Neumann algebra 𝔐 on a Hilbert space with a unit vector Ω that is cyclic (𝔐Ω is dense) and separating (aΩ = 0 implies a = 0). The vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory is cyclic and separating for every local algebra (Reeh–Schlieder). The antilinear map S: aΩ ↦ a*Ω is closable; its closure has polar decomposition S = JΔ^{1/2}, with Δ positive self-adjoint (the modular operator) and J an antiunitary involution (the modular conjugation). Tomita–Takesaki: Δ^{it}𝔐Δ^{−it} = 𝔐 and J𝔐J = 𝔐′. Since SΩ = Ω and JΩ = Ω, ΔΩ = Ω. That is the whole content of "the self-referential state": a theorem about any cyclic separating vector. It is not an image of self-existence, of mind, or of the ground (§6, items 4, 9, 17).

**11.2 Modular flow as candidate time.** σ_t(a) = Δ^{it} a Δ^{−it} is a one-parameter group of automorphisms of 𝔐: intrinsic (nothing external enters), canonical (the unique flow satisfying the KMS condition for ω at inverse temperature one — theorem), and state-preserving (ω∘σ_t = ω, so the state is stationary while the observables flow). Its generator K = −log Δ is formally a Heisenberg-picture Hamiltonian. The **thermal time hypothesis** (Connes–Rovelli 1994) identifies the modular parameter of the physical state with physical time. Register: **conjecture** — with one exact geometric instance and two nontrivial checks. The instance: for a Rindler wedge in Minkowski space the modular flow of the vacuum is the Lorentz boost (Bisognano–Wichmann 1975), the modular parameter is Rindler time, and the KMS condition is the Unruh effect. The checks: for the cosmic background radiation state the thermal time coincides with cosmological time (Rovelli 1993); for a finite-lifetime observer the thermal time of a double cone yields a temperature with the expected Unruh limit (Martinetti–Rovelli 2003). Type structure is not optional: in a thermofield-double state whose mixing operators close under multiplication, the algebra is type III₁ (Furuya–Lashkari–Moosa–Ouseph 2023) — forced by thermal mixing, not by metaphysics. The conjecture carries its critics: Swanson (2021) presses the thermal-time/proper-time relation and the classical implementation; Chua (2025) the circularity worry — not every one-parameter automorphism group deserves to be read as time translation, and securing that reading seems to need dynamical assumptions the hypothesis was meant to supply (§22.10). Neither touches the theorem (Tomita–Takesaki needs no dynamics); both touch the *identification*, which is why §9.4 enters the modular flow as "parametrized, not oriented" and as output of (algebra, state) rather than as time.

**The measure reading (M2), which is exact.** A faithful normal state ω on 𝔐 is a noncommutative probability measure — on a commutative algebra L^∞(X, μ) the normal states are exactly the probability measures absolutely continuous with respect to μ. Tomita–Takesaki says: *the measure determines a flow.* Connes's cocycle theorem (1973) says: for two faithful normal states, σ_t^φ(a) = u_t σ_t^ω(a) u_t* with u_t = (Dφ:Dω)_t the **noncommutative Radon–Nikodym derivative** — the change of measure is the change of clock — and consequently the composite ℝ → Aut(𝔐) → Out(𝔐) is independent of the state: *the algebra alone fixes the flow up to inner automorphisms; the state fixes the rest.* On a commutative algebra every state is tracial and the flow is trivial: a classical measure generates no time. Time-from-measure is a strictly noncommutative phenomenon, which is the point Connes and Rovelli made in their title. This is the Lighthouse's "measure precedes duration" in its sharpest available form: **law (algebra) → duration up to gauge; measure (state) → which clock.** It is a translation of the heuristic into a theorem, not a derivation of physics from the heuristic.

**11.3 Clocks as modular flows; the cocycle as the transformation between clocks.** A clock is a subsystem with a state, (𝔑, ω_𝔑), with its own modular flow; equivalence classes of such pairs correspond to conjugacy classes of modular flows (theorem, via Tomita–Takesaki). The clock ambiguity of §9.3 becomes "which modular flow is the time?" The cocycles between all pairs of states form a groupoid; the reading that the cocycle is a gauge field, the absolute flow gauge-dependent, and the relative modular structure the invariant content, is an **interpretation** of a theorem, and is kept as such. Two statements in v2.0 are corrected here. First, "the Connes–Takesaki theorem makes the unitary group act transitively on faithful normal states" is not correct as an exact statement: the result is **Connes–Størmer homogeneity** (1978) — a factor is type III₁ if and only if for any two faithful normal states and any ε > 0 there is a unitary u with ‖φ∘Ad u − ψ‖ < ε, i.e. transitivity holds in norm closure, approximately. (The Connes–Takesaki flow of weights is a different object; for III₁ it is trivial.) "Exactly one physical state up to gauge" is therefore not even available as an interpretation of an exact theorem. Second, and more important, the gauge reading is contradicted at the level that matters by §11.4: in the gravitational algebras, changing the clock changes the algebra.

**11.4 The crossed product: the clock inside the algebra (2022–2025).** The v2.0 plan asked how a clock could be inserted into a timeless algebraic description with the gravitational constraint respected. That has been done, semiclassically, and the results change the honest status of three of the plan's questions.

- **Witten 2022.** For a large-N holographic theory in the thermofield-double (black-hole) state, the boundary single-trace algebra is type III₁ (Leutheusser–Liu 2021–23, who also show that half-sided modular inclusions of that algebra generate an *emergent time* in the black-hole interior). Adjoining the generator of time translations — i.e. passing to the **crossed product 𝔐 ⋊_σ ℝ of the algebra by its own modular flow** — yields a type II_∞ algebra with a trace, hence a renormalized entropy. Chandrasekaran–Penington–Witten 2023: that entropy is the generalized entropy.
- **Chandrasekaran–Longo–Penington–Witten 2023.** In the static patch of de Sitter space, with an observer consisting of nothing but a clock whose Hamiltonian is bounded below, the algebra of observables gravitationally dressed to the observer's worldline is a **type II₁** factor. It has a trace defined on all of its elements, hence entropies for all states; it has a **maximum-entropy state** — empty de Sitter (the Bunch–Davies state) tensored with a simple state of the observer — whose density matrix is the identity; and the entropy of any semiclassical state agrees, up to a state-independent constant, with the generalized entropy A/4G + S_out. Without the observer, the constraint leaves no nontrivial observables at all.
- **2025–26 (added 15 October 2026, seventh amendment; §22.10).** Imposing the *full* de Sitter isometry constraints — so that the observer's geodesic itself fluctuates — gives an averaged modular crossed product over static patches, still type II with a faithful normal trace, type II₁ once the clock Hamiltonian is bounded below, with an observer-dependent entropy matching the generalized entropy for semiclassical states (Chen–Xu, arXiv:2511.00622; preprint). The angle-dependent horizon-area fluctuations ("boost supertranslation" charges) impose infinitely many further constraints and the dressed algebra remains type II — II_∞ with no maximum-entropy state for black holes, II₁ for de Sitter once the observer is modelled realistically with rotational and binding energy (Klinger–Kudler-Flam–Satishchandran 2026, PRD). The three quantum-reference-frame approaches that underlie DEHK are related to one another in De Vuyst–Höhn–Tsobanjan, Quantum 10 (2026) 2196. None of this alters the plan's reading: the clock or observer is the condition of existence of the algebra, the clock's choice is physical, and P15 is open.
- **Jensen–Sorce–Speranza 2023**: the construction for general subregions with an observer. **Witten 2023**: a background-independent algebra along an observer's worldline. **Chen–Penington 2024** ("A clock is just a way to tell the time"): gravitational algebras in cosmological spacetimes, with the argument that, within their assumptions, the algebra obtained does not depend on which clock is used. **Kudler-Flam–Leutheusser–Satishchandran 2024**: algebraic observational cosmology — observers in expanding universes.
- **De Vuyst–Eccles–Höhn–Kirklin 2024–25.** The observers in these constructions are quantum reference frames in the Trinity's sense; a proper accounting of gauge invariance and observer degrees of freedom converts the type III algebra to a type II crossed product; and **different QRFs give different algebras and different entropies** — gravitational entropy is observer-dependent, with explicit examples (a gravitational interferometer; degenerate clock superselection; monotonic versus periodic clocks). **De Vuyst–Höhn–Tsobanjan 2025** relate the perspective-neutral, algebraic, and effective QRF formalisms. **Fewster–Janssen–Loveridge–Rejzner–Waldron 2024**: QRF dressing changes the type of local algebras in QFT measurement schemes. **Faulkner–Speranza 2024**: the generalized second law in gravitational algebras.
- **2025–26, the clock need not be added by hand (added 15 October 2026, tenth amendment; §22.13; §23.2 item 100).** Three constructions build the clock from the physics rather than adjoining an external observer. *Speranza (CQG 42 (2025) 215023):* in the Chen–Penington slow-roll example the observer is constructed from the quantum fields themselves; the gravitational algebra is the Connes–Takesaki flow of weights presented as a crossed product, decomposing into field and observer degrees of freedom; **a change of observer is a change of quantum reference frame**, and every semiclassical type II construction so far is "the result of gauging modular flow." *Freidel–Kirklin (JHEP 2026, 130):* the degrees of freedom on a gravitational null ray quantized with the **dressing time** — a QRF made of the gravitational field itself, covering the full diffeomorphism group along the ray — giving a Virasoro crossed product. *Blommaert–Chen (arXiv:2602.22153; preprint):* in JT gravity, a subregion fixed by the dilaton value; the gravitational constraint that generates **York-time** evolution inside the causal diamond is used in a crossed product, the extrinsic curvature being the physical clock; observables are dressed to features of the spacetime, with no external observer; and the entropy of the resulting algebra is *not* an area or any boundary term, because York flow is a conformal isometry rather than an isometry — repeated for Milne-type closed Big-Bang universes. Also: horizon-cut algebras as crossed products by half-sided supertranslations from doubled corner edge modes, type II_∞ per cut (Chandrasekaran–Flanagan 2026, preprint); gravitational dressing as a generalization of the crossed product (Giddings 2025, preprint). *A caveat at finite times:* beyond the scrambling time, shockwave corrections break the KMS condition for static-patch vacuum correlators, and with the clock quantized the Hartle–Hawking state is **not a trace** on the crossed product (Cui–Kolchmeyer 2026, preprint) — so the CLPW maximum-entropy state, and every type-II statement of this section, is a leading-order statement (G18). *Typicality survives the passage to type II:* the Lubkin–Page bounds — generic states have vanishing subsystem correlations — hold for all type II factors, with exponential suppression in S_BH for the II_∞ gravitational algebras, and fail for type III (Wang–Braunstein, PRD 113 (2026) L121904); the fact changes when the clock is included, again (§6, item 6; G12).

**Consequences for the plan** (all entered in §15, §19, §20).

1. *The type question, honestly* (replacing v2.0 §7.4): the local algebras of quantum field theory on a background are hyperfinite type III₁; once the gravitational constraint and a clock are included, the algebra is type II (II_∞ with a boundary time; II₁ for a bounded-below observer Hamiltonian in de Sitter), semiclassically, to leading order in G. The "resonance" v2.0 found between non-duality and type III₁ was with an algebra that lacked its clock.
2. *The clock is inserted with the constraint.* These constructions are Page–Wootters in von Neumann-algebraic form, with gravity — the first setting in which §9's open item 4 has a worked instance.
3. *The clock ambiguity is not pure gauge* at this level: the algebra and its entropies depend on the clock (DEHK), while in some cosmological settings they do not (Chen–Penington). When the gravitational algebra is clock-independent is **P15**, and the entropy difference between clock choices is the physical distortion measure that WP2 lacked. *Tenth amendment:* P15 now has two halves — (i) must a clock be added by hand? answered in the negative by construction (Speranza; Blommaert–Chen; Freidel–Kirklin); (ii) does the algebra, its entropy, and (non-perturbatively) the very dimension of the Hilbert space depend on which clock or observer? yes in DEHK, no in Chen–Penington, yes for closed universes (§9.3 item 2), and yes for the *form* of the entropy — an area term with an isometric clock, bulk terms with a York clock (Blommaert–Chen). Half (ii) is the open question; T2′ ("area term from the trace") inherits the caveat that the area form is clock-dependent.
4. *A selection principle supplied by the algebra.* In type II₁ the trace is a state and it is the maximum-entropy state. That is a distinguished state the algebra itself provides. It is the *wrong end* of the arrow (§11.6), but it is the first state-selection fact in the programme that is a theorem.
5. *The relational and modular layers are bridged.* v2.0's glue "modular theory ↔ holography" was "established in special cases"; the glue the plan actually needed — **quantum reference frames ↔ crossed products** (GL-6) — is now partly established (DEHK; DHT). WP0 is added to absorb it.

**11.5 The standard form, and space.** The standard form (𝔐, ℋ, J, 𝒫) — algebra, Hilbert space, modular conjugation, self-dual natural cone — is unique up to unitary equivalence; faithful normal states correspond bijectively to vectors in the cone's interior (Araki–Connes); the algebra is recoverable from the cone. It is a theorem that algebra and state are two aspects of one structure *in this mathematical sense*. Nothing about the ground follows; the row "the standard form is the Absolute" is struck (§8). The emergent-space material is carried at the status v2.0's retraction fixed: a Connes-type distance on the state space as a candidate spatial metric, the cocycle as a connection, the reduction to a Bures or Fisher metric — interpretation or speculation; the modular-inclusion and crossed-product programme (Wiesbrock's half-sided modular inclusions; Liu 2025; Leutheusser–Liu's emergent times), entanglement-based geometry (van Raamsdonk 2010; Ryu–Takayanagi 2006), and Jacobson's 1995 derivation of the Einstein equation as an equation of state given the area law and the Unruh temperature — active research with partial results, not a completed derivation.

**11.6 State selection, repaired.** v2.0 §7.6 proposed three layered selection principles and named the second "the Past Hypothesis layer: the state of minimal modular energy — the modular ground state, with modular Hamiltonian positive semidefinite — whose positivity would give the flow a preferred direction." v2.0 §6.2 had already retracted "the Past-Hypothesis synthesis" as invented before §7.6 reinstated it. It must stay retracted, for a reason that is a theorem:

> **Proposition 11.1 (no modular ground state on a type III algebra).** For a faithful normal state ω on a von Neumann algebra, JΔJ = Δ⁻¹, so the spectrum of Δ is invariant under λ ↦ 1/λ. Hence K = −log Δ ≥ 0 ⇔ Δ ≤ 1 ⇔ Δ ≥ 1 ⇔ Δ = 1 ⇔ σ_t = id ⇔ ω is a trace. Type III algebras admit no faithful normal trace; tracial *states* exist only in types I_n and II₁. In type III₁ the spectrum of K is all of ℝ for every faithful normal state (Connes spectrum ℝ₊). Therefore no state on the algebras of §11.1–11.3 has a positive semidefinite modular Hamiltonian, and "minimal modular energy" has no minimizer.

So the Past-Hypothesis layer, T2 ("area law for the modular ground state"), and gaps G8 and G11 are void as written. The repair uses §11.4. In the clock-dressed **type II₁** algebra a trace exists and *is* a state; but it is the **maximum-entropy** state — in de Sitter, empty de Sitter — i.e. the equilibrium end, not the low-entropy beginning. The arrow therefore cannot come from modular positivity. It can only come from a state of **low entropy relative to the trace**, which is a *contingent* selection. Under the non-dual assumption that was an embarrassment; under the Lighthouse it is what one expects of a created order, and the demand on it is D-8's: the selection must be lawful — derivable, not brute (§17). The generalized second law in gravitational algebras (Faulkner–Speranza 2024) then supplies the arrow *in form* — entropy relative to the trace does not decrease — while the low-entropy state supplies it *in fact*.

The three layers, restated:

| Layer | Question | Mechanism | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Structural** — geometric modular action | What form must the state have? | The modular flow acts geometrically on a net of subalgebras. Instances: the Minkowski vacuum for wedges (Bisognano–Wichmann); conformal double cones (Hislop–Longo); the Bunch–Davies state for the de Sitter static patch, which is KMS at the Gibbons–Hawking temperature for the static-patch flow | Theorem in the instances; conjecture in general (T1; WP4) |
| **Contingency** — the arrow (replaces "Past Hypothesis as modular ground state") | Why is there a direction? | A state of low entropy relative to the trace of the clock-dressed type II algebra; the generalized second law gives monotonicity | The trace is a theorem (type II₁); the low-entropy selection is contingent and must be lawful (D-8); its cosmological form is open (§17; G19) |
| **Emergence** — decoherence | How does classicality arise? | Pointer bases; approximate records; observer agreement | Standard; time-symmetric; extension to the modular setting open (G16) |

**Consolidated candidate** (conjecture at every joint): the physical state has geometric modular action on an emergent net, has low entropy relative to the trace of its clock-dressed algebra, and has reduced states that decohere. The four classical-time features no layer yet delivers — a single shared parameter, Lorentzian signature, the Schrödinger equation for general subsystems, the Einstein equations — are atoms in §15.
