|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| N-1 | **Predictive processing, rigorously bounded** — how far does prediction-error minimization actually reach, stated at its proven scope? | Logic: precision of claim; evidence: partial support in perception and motor control | Bounded claims with explicit failure reports; adversarial collaborations | The framework's core proves unfalsifiable in practice (every anomaly absorbed as "precision weighting") | Unchanged. *Note (item 75):* a proven-scope statement now has a formal register — bounded predictors as finite-state machines with budget M; shortfall Δ(M), thresholds κ_det / N* / rank H, identification cost Θ(M log M) (clue 8; §10.6 addendum). Vocabulary, not support. *Scope note (item 95, ninth amendment):* the predictive-processing framework presupposes records — its priors are traces — and therefore presupposes the thermodynamic arrow (§17.1(a)); it bears on the *manifest* present and flow (Hartle 2005; §9.3 item 5), never on the arrow's existence. A "the arrow is in the brain" claim is excluded by N-1's own ledger. |
| N-2 | **Interoception and the self-model** — what is the minimal architecture of self-related processing, and what does it not explain? | Evidence: active literature; logic: the self-model framework is precisely stateable | Dissociations isolating self-model layers; tests separating self-model theory from rivals | No experiment separates self-model accounts from global-workspace accounts | Unchanged |
| N-3 | **The structure of integration (the bridge, P5)** — what licenses a claim about *consciousness* here, and what independently licenses a bridge between structure and experience? | Logic: the double blockage; evidence: adversarial collaborations on the main theories returned mixed support | Organizational/reflexive candidates tested against their own burden (e.g. the combination problem); one independently warranted bridge claim | Any move from structure to experience without naming the bridge premise | The only licensed near-side form of the struck glue GL-3 |
| N-4 | **Quantum biology, at its proven scale** — where is quantum coherence actually functional in biology? | Evidence: genuine cases (e.g. radical-pair magnetoreception); contested cases (olfactory tunneling) flagged | Functional-coherence results surviving decoherence bounds and controls; replication | Robust evidence of *functional* quantum processing in warm neural tissue would falsify N-3's pruning — stated in advance | D-5 |
| N-5 | **Dissociation battery: intake ↔ inference, poles of one act** — if sensing and intuition are poles of one act, breakdown cases (perception without integration; integration without input) should be systematically dissociable and mutually informing | Logic: precise structure; Qur'an: 16:78, 7:179 read as integration claims, compatible; evidence: existing paradigms | A small battery of four dissociation families with pre-registered predictions about bidirectional degradation | The poles prove independent — failures dissociate cleanly with no mutual degradation | D-6 |
| N-6 | **Constraint as an explanatory currency** — are organizational variables ever irreducible in successful explanations of life and mind? | Logic: precise claim; evidence: case analyses; Qur'an: lawful-world constraint | Project D: a survey of at least 20 completed reductions, tallying genuine remainders | A clean, remainder-free reduction of a central case | D-1; project D (reduction-remainder survey) lives here |
| N-7 | **Reality monitoring and the attribution of interior influence** — independent of any metaphysics of its source, is there a lawful science of how the brain attributes internal signals to self vs. world, and what happens when attribution fails? | Evidence: **the gap is real and measured** — unconscious processing demonstrably influences behaviour without awareness (masked priming; blindsight; dorsal-stream guidance of action dissociated from conscious perception, e.g. the hollow-mask flicking paradigm — with a live dispute over whether the affected processes are "genuinely unconscious" or degraded conscious vision, which is itself informative); conscious vision is slow and plausibly tied to reality-monitoring functions (Fleming & Michel, *BBS* 2026, with 34 commentaries contesting details); logic: sharply stateable; Qur'an: neutral — the text locates the *source* on the far side (P13); this question studies the near side | Paradigms separating source-*monitoring* from source-*identification*; pre-registered predictions about attribution failure (agency errors, intrusive-thought phenomena) mapping onto distinct processing signatures; one dissociation showing unattributed influence with no sensory registration | No lawful structure exists — attribution failures prove unsystematic and modality-bound, adding nothing over general arousal/attention accounts. **Note:** the question is about the opening; what may be on the far side is outside its jurisdiction (P13) | The only licensed near-side form of P13; D-2's vocabulary audit applies |

*Open questions P1–P14, with v1.0's content columns.* (Where this table and the status table above differ, the status table governs.)

| ID | Question | Why open | Best current candidates | What would fill it | Guards | v2.1/v2.2 update |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **P1** | The nature of the ground | A declared boundary (17:85); *bilā kayf*; logic constrains but does not construct | — (constraints only: non-composite, necessary, not a member of the world) | Clarified constraint sets that survive logic and the text; **never** a mechanism | No object-modelling (dead end 1); the ground grounds, it is not identical with what it grounds | Guard added (v2.1): no structure of this document is a candidate |
| **P2** | Time's emergence | No worked reconstruction yet | Page–Wootters; causal sets; loop quantum gravity; quantum reference frames; the measure-before-duration reading (§4.2) is the framework's preferred shape | A construction recovering all three required features (RQ-1) | Must not need a hidden preferred time; measure-first is a reading, not a derivation | Restated (v2.1): see the status table; D-14 |
| **P3** | The measure / why these laws | A severe open problem | Landscape plus measure; necessity; initial-condition constraints; the plenitude reading (with guard) | A measure with consequences; a verdict on the Boltzmann-brain issue; parameters derivable from the measure structure (D-9) | A measure that predicts nothing does not fill it. **And plenitude alone fills nothing** (dead end 12): if all values are realized somewhere, no value of our level is thereby explained | Joined to P2 under Conjecture M (v2.1) |
| **P4** | The classical limit | Formulations diverge | Decoherence plus einselection; collapse models (constrained — and shrinking: see D-7 and RQ-8) | A principled boundary with discriminating tests | A boundary that moves with each experiment is fitting, not filling | Unchanged |
| **P5** | The bridge from structure to experience | Blocked from both directions; mixed results from adversarial collaborations | Organizational/reflexive candidates, each with its burden (e.g. the combination problem); the participationist dual-aspect reading — relational and intrinsic aspects of one informational structure, the intrinsic received and grounded | An independently warranted bridge claim that survives its own burden | No bridge from convenience; no verdict by fiat. **The dual-aspect reading does not fill this** — it presupposes two-aspect structure, graded participation, and the received character of the intrinsic, none of them independently established; it stays a heuristic until warranted | Unchanged; §4.2 states the dual-aspect reading and its burden |
| **P6** | The gap between facts and norms | Is/ought; a norm of truth cannot be derived from facts | Theistic grounding of norm; realist options | A grounding account that collapses into neither fact nor stipulation | No "ought" from physics; no stipulation dressed as derivation | Unchanged |
| **P7** | The grammar of polarity | The axes are stated, not yet formalized | Logics of ground (Fine); adjunctions; duality classification | A calculus in which constituting polarities are representable and *do work* — classify and predict | A formalism that only re-describes adds nothing; and the one-idea-three-views check stands (§4, 'The internal check') | Progressed (v2.1): trade-off and adjunction tests |
| **P8** | The silence | Nothing outside a theory can decide it; no fixed meaning of an absence | The tradition's replies (all judged insufficient by the one who raises the complaint) | Nothing available; record the dilemma, the reason, and the date | Neither assert presence nor absence. Point of record: the silence follows from several readings alike — it does not choose between them | Unchanged |
| **P9** | Quantum gravity and unification | No empirical contact | Strings; loops; asymptotic safety; causal sets; scorecard under a stated criterion (fit to measure-first, information-first, emergent-time structure): holography and causal sets lead; loop quantum gravity, entropic gravity, group-field theory, causal dynamical triangulations, twistor theory mid; perturbative strings and asymptotic safety trail. Expected: the programmes are complementary views of one informational structure | Any empirical signature; cosmological consistency; the complementarity claim becoming a derivation | Compatibility alone never counts; a scorecard without its criterion is inadmissible; "complementary views" is a reading until it generates a discriminating test | Re-verdicted (v2.1, §13): scorecard criterion-relative |
| **P10** | The dictionary from gradation to measurement | If gradation-talk is to touch physics, the translation is missing | None yet. **The plenitude reading makes this urgent**: if all radii are actualized, identifying *which* level is ours — and deriving its constants — becomes the load-bearing question | A stated map from gradation claims to measurable structure | Without it, no physical prediction may be claimed from the identity reading; no level-identification by convenience | Still missing (v2.1) |
| **P11** | The dark sector | Standard physics open question | Particle or field candidates; modified gravity | Detection or decisive dynamics | Keep separate from the ground — **and from the unseen orders** (no dark-sector-as-jinn moves) | Unchanged |
| **P13** | The unseen orders (jinn, angels; the *rūḥ* separately at 17:85) | The text affirms them — real, willful, accountable, largely imperceptible (51:56; 6:130; surah 72; 7:27) — and does not locate them spatially, compositionally, or detectably. Physics reads the visible side; they are on the far side | Structural location only: a level of the layered cosmos (heavens in layers, 67:3; 71:15; the stars are in the *first* heaven, 41:12, 67:5, 37:6 — which is why the classical commentaries read stacked domains, not parallel universes) | **Nothing physical** — by construction. The studyable part is the near side (N-7) | No identification with any physical sector (dead end 13); no denial by omission either (2:3, "who believe in the unseen"); no mechanism or instrument claims. The imperceptibility is asymmetric, not absolute (7:27), and the warning (72:6) targets the human act — invocation, alliance, service — which lies on our side | Unchanged in status. *Restored detail from the Master Synthesis §VIII (item 59):* the jinn are created from smokeless fire (55:15); they see you and you do not see them (7:27); they reach the interior (114:4–6); seeking refuge in them increases burden (72:6) — the warning is "do not call", not "do not touch"; the asymmetry of access is one-directional, and physics reads the near side only. "Angels created from light" is a hadith (Muslim 2996), not a verse — cited by status (§6, what to do, 13) |
| **P14** | Does creation follow necessarily from God's goodness? | The text gives the terms — God complete (112:2), God loving (*al-Raḥmān* 55:1; *al-Wadūd* 11:90, 85:14), creation free and purposive (21:16; 5:17) — and no resolution | Reason's candidates each pay a price: an internal object of love (Trinitarian — costs simplicity, and closed for this framework); necessity of love (costs simplicity); denial of the inference (the good is self-diffusive, yet creation remains free — whether this is stable or collapses into voluntarism is disputed); voluntarism (creation arbitrary — costs the grounding of creation in God's nature); plenitude (no selection — costs neither freedom nor contingency on the classical concepts, but explains nothing about our level) | What would fill it: a derivation showing that one of these costs is not a cost. What the text does support: the preference is real, free, and grounded in abundance rather than need. Why there is manifestation at all remains the open question | No uniqueness claims; the tension is real, but closing it is not established; no scoreboard against other traditions — each pays, differently (the Trinity trades one puzzle for another); the Hidden Treasure saying carries no weight (§6, what to do, item 13) | Unchanged; the plenitude premise (classical freedom as self-determination, not indifference; the good as self-diffusive) is one of the recorded candidates and remains a reading, not a premise (§6, item 13) |

## §20 Gap register G1 – G20

A gap is registered whenever a load-bearing step is missing, analogical-only, unverified, or not yet well-posed; each carries a fill or the statement that none is claimed. v2.0's sixteen are preserved with their numbers; four are added.

| # | Gap | Severity | Status / fill in v2.1 |
|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | The no-adjoint obstruction was manuscript-reported; its transfer to gravity is analogical | Structural (was Blocking) | *Downgraded in v2.2:* the finite-dimensional obstruction is now Theorem 10.2, re-derived here; what remains analogical is the transfer to algebraic and gravitational categories (Conjecture C-NOADJ′; WP3). The "intercept principle" as an epistemic limit is withdrawn by the manuscript itself (dead end 17) |
| G2 | Ideal-clock idealization of conditional dynamics | Local | WP1 (finite-precision clocks; back-reaction; decoherence). *Sharpened (tenth amendment):* the idealization fails first in the measurement model (Hausmann–Schmidhuber–Castro-Ruiz 2025); rod–clock resolution trade-off for a single space-time QRF (Mattei–Castro-Ruiz 2025) |
| G3 | The sphere analogy presupposes an ambient space | Structural | **Resolved as a formal matter** (§10.2): the ambient is the slice category and the adjoint exists; the physical content is relocated to the normalized layer |
| G4 | No physical distortion measure for the rate–distortion shell | Structural | **Candidate found:** the entropy difference between clock choices on the clock-dressed algebra (DEHK); WP2 tests it |
| G5 | Semantic gap between process categories and algebraic QFT | Structural | WP3 translation manual; sharpened by Rev. 2.8: the finite-dimensional proofs use extreme points and dimension counts that have no type III analogue, so the manual must say what replaces them (candidate: the orthogonal-program argument of C.5) |
| G6 | Constraint algebras with structure functions resist the Trinity machinery | Structural | Scope restricted to constant algebras; deformation analysis listed (§9) |
| G7 | Type III₁ selection claim is interpretation, not derivation | Structural | **Replaced** (§11.4, consequence 1): the type is a physics question; III₁ on a background, II with constraint and clock. The "resonance" is struck |
| G8 | The three-layer state-selection synthesis is conjectural at every joint | Blocking | **Restated** (§11.6): structural layer → T1/WP4; contingency layer → A9; emergence layer → G16 |
| G9 | Modular flow to Schrödinger: cocycle derivative may not exist | Blocking | WP5, with the crossed product as the setting; explicit go/no-go |
| G10 | Locality of the modular Hamiltonian beyond wedges and spheres | Blocking | WP4 (T1); negative result accepted as publishable. *Now explicit in examples (tenth amendment):* local-plus-non-local generators for null-bounded regions (Lu–Wang 2025); deformed half-spaces (Ali–Suneeta 2024); WP4 starts there |
| G11 | Area law for the modular ground state unproved | Structural | **Void** (Proposition 11.1): no such state exists. Replaced by T2′ (area term from the trace) |
| G12 | Born rule from the modular measure not established | Structural | **Restated** (§16.3): the Born measure is unique by Gleason/Busch (theorem); open is the relational conditioning map (A4) and the typicality step beyond Bohmian mechanics. *Tenth amendment:* the typicality step has its type II foundation (Wang–Braunstein 2026: Lubkin–Page bounds for all type II factors, failing for type III); A4 unchanged |
| G13 | Constants from modular invariants is a placeholder | Local | PH-2 kept unreformulated; T3 the only route |
| G14 | Relational actualization lacks a definiteness map | Structural | **Gloss overridden** (§8; §16.4); the physics remainder — the conditioning-to-record map via decoherence — stays in WP7 |
| G15 | Lorentzian signature from a Connes-type distance is open | Structural | **Candidate fill, unevaluated (tenth amendment):** Lorentzian signature emerging from a Riemannian *twisted* spectral triple within the almost-commutative structure of the noncommutative Standard Model, as an alternative to Wick rotation (Nieuviarts, IJGMMP 2026; arXiv:2502.18105). Not evaluated here; until it is, the gap stands. *Noted:* in the crossed-product constructions signature is inherited from the background, so the gap stands for any background-free version |
| G16 | Observer agreement in the classical limit unanalyzed; extension of decoherence to the modular setting | Local | WP5/WP7: cocycle triviality under coarse-graining |
| **G17** (new) | The tensor-product split into clock and rest is not given by the state; any selection principle ("quantum mereology," Carroll–Singh 2021) is a measure-from-state principle, and Theorem 10.2 shows the split cannot be undone inside the normalized theory by currying | Structural | WP1 (finite models); WP6 (frames on causal orders); no fill claimed for the general case. *Tenth amendment:* the split's non-perturbative cousin — a closed universe without an observer has a one-dimensional Hilbert space, with an observer one of dimension ≈ e^{S_Ob} (Harlow–Usatyuk–Zhao 2026; Abdalla–Antonini–Iliesiu–Levine 2025; prescriptions differ, Akers et al. 2025) — §9.3 item 2; the selecting principle is still not supplied. *(v2.1 used "G17" for both this gap and the Int/Ext poset vocabulary; the latter is not a gap and lives at dead end 15 only — resolved in v2.2.)* |
| **G18** (new) | The modular layer was written before the 2022–25 crossed-product results; integration is incomplete, and every §11.4 statement is at leading semiclassical order | Structural | WP0; A1 restated; until done, the plan's type-II statements carry the qualifier "at leading order". *The qualifier now has a concrete failure mode (tenth amendment):* beyond the scrambling time the KMS condition breaks and, with the clock quantized, the Hartle–Hawking state is not a trace on the crossed product (Cui–Kolchmeyer 2026, preprint) |
| **G19** (new) | No modular ground state; the distinguished state of the clock-dressed type II₁ algebra is maximum entropy; the low-entropy selection relative to the trace is open | Blocking | A9; §17.2; if the selection proves brute, D-8 fails for the arrow. *Epistemic ceiling (tenth amendment):* the vector within the low-entropy subspace is not empirically selectable (Chen–Tumulka 2024); A9 is a claim about the subspace |
| **G20** (new) | The absoluteness cost: with absolute outcomes and no collapse, actualization is nonlocal and contextual (Bell; KS; Bong et al.), the state is ontic (PBR), and the account is a hidden-variable theory that owes the Born distribution | Structural | Registered, not filled (§16). The Lighthouse pays with emergent locality; A4 and the typicality step are the owed items |

**Deliberate exclusions** (v2.0 §12.3, retained with one change): the metaphysics of becoming (undecidable by the plan's methods; causal-set growth is the one mechanism on offer); consciousness (P5, N-3; GL-3/GL-4/PH-1 struck); and — *changed* — the "dissolution" framing is no longer an excluded *option* but a forbidden *move* (§6, item 16).

## §21 Dead ends

Entered with the reason and the reopening condition. "Never" means: not in a document governed by the Lighthouse, absent new evidence of the kind stated in v1.0 §8.

| # | Dead end | Reason | Reopen |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The non-dual working assumption as a premise of the plan | Overridden by the Lighthouse (real distinction; 42:11; 112) | Never, in this document |
| 2 | "The standard form is the Absolute"; "the modular fixed point is self-existence"; "type III₁ is the non-dual algebra" | §6, items 4, 6, 9; and the type changes when the clock is included | Never |
| 3 | The dissolution track; structural circularity as a requirement | §6, items 16, 18 | Never |
| 4 | "Past Hypothesis = modular ground state"; T2; G11 | Proposition 11.1 — no positive modular Hamiltonian on any type III algebra; the type II₁ trace is maximum entropy | Never (theorem) |
| 5 | An arrow from the direction of the modular flow itself | The modular flow of the commutant (𝔐′, Ω) is the time-reverse (its modular operator is Δ⁻¹); the formalism prefers no direction | Never (theorem). The arrow needs a low-entropy state (A9) |
| 6 | C-NOADJ in its geometric form: "the functor X ↦ X × ℝ has no adjoint" | Proposition 10.1 — the right adjoint exists and is Newtonian time | Never (theorem); the normalized form survives as the restated conjecture |
| 7 | "Exact transitivity of the unitary group on states" (misattributed to Connes–Takesaki) | Connes–Størmer 1978 gives norm-density, i.e. approximate transitivity, for III₁ | Never as an exact statement |
| 8 | The clock ambiguity as pure gauge | DEHK: a change of clock changes the gravitational algebra and its entropy | Reopens only if P15 resolves to general clock-independence. *Reinforced (tenth amendment):* non-perturbatively, even the Hilbert-space dimension of a closed universe depends on how the observer is included (HUZ vs AAIL; §9.3 item 2) |

| 10 | The non-dual filter's verdicts on quantum-gravity programmes (v2.0 §8.4) | Verdicts by metaphysics are not grounds (§13.3) | Never as verdicts; each programme is re-assessed by D-7/8/9/11/14 |
| 11 | The remark associating conformal cyclic cosmology with 21:104 | §6, item 3 (verse-to-theory matching) | Never |
| 12 | "Measure precedes duration" *derived* from 54:49 | The verse anchors vocabulary (M1); the physics instances (§12.1) are the evidence | Never as evidence; the heuristic stays [H] |
| 13 | Fields-only ontology as a solution to measurement | It supplies neither a time nor a definiteness map (§13.2) | Never |

## §22 Citations

**Protocol.** Three statuses. **Verified this session** — the arXiv identifier and journal reference were checked against the publisher's or arXiv's record during the 14 October 2026 audit. **Standard — verify journal reference** — a well-known result whose arXiv identifier has been machine-checked (15 October 2026) but whose journal details may still be from memory and must be checked before any external use. **Unverified** — an unpublished or unrefereed source, quarantined. No status is promoted without a check.

**22.3 Unrefereed manuscript — main text independently re-derived.** A. Abaee, *The Opfibration Ontology: Quantum Instruments, Irreversibility, and the Epistemic Asymptote*, Rev. 2.8 (October 2026; v2.0's [10] in its mature form; user-supplied as clue 5; companion repositories `MIKEAA2020/opfibration-merged-`, `MIKEAA2020/Quantum-combs`; software record Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.20860298 — a software deposit, not the manuscript, as the manuscript itself notes). Status: unpublished and unrefereed; the main-text theorems (Prop. 3.1; Thms 4.3, 4.5, 4.6, 4.8, 4.12; Props 5.1, 5.5, 6.1, 6.2; Thm 5.2; Thm C.2; Prop. C.35) were re-derived symbolically and numerically on 14 October 2026 — 20/20 checks, `v2.2/verify/opfibration_checks.py` and `.log`. Not re-derived: Appendix C's quotient-category and infinite-dimensional results (C.5–C.28) and the programming benchmarks (C.29–C.34). Used in §9, §10, §14, §15, §17, §20, §21. Open question R7 (§7.2) adjudicated later the same day in the narrowed form stated in §10.4 — no fixed-grade A-natural representation on the non-degenerate locus, the bodies differing already at A = ℂ — with 15/15 checks in `v2.2/verify/r7_check.py` and `.log`; the stronger claim (non-isomorphism for every A) and R1–R6, R8 remain open.

**22.5 Qur'anic text and literature consulted for §4.1 (14 October 2026).** *Text (verified — machine-checked against the downloaded corpus):* Qur'an, Uthmani-simple Arabic (Tanzil edition) with Sahih International, Arberry and Pickthall, retrieved through the alquran.cloud API into `v2.2/verify/quran/`; all verse citations in §4.1 and all counts (24 lā-yahdī verses with God as subject, 13 sealing-of-hearts verses, 33 law-shāʾa verses, 6 khāliq-kulli-shayʾ verses, 18 "wronged themselves", 17 + 19 "God does not wrong" / "they shall not be wronged") were produced by `v2.2/verify/quran/counts.py` (with `q.py`) on that corpus. *Exegesis (consulted in English; verify against the Arabic before external use):* Tafsīr al-Jalālayn and Ibn Kathīr on 37:96 (both readings of *mā*; Ibn Kathīr: "synonymous"); Mawdūdī, Tafhīm al-Qurʾān on 7:179 (note 140, consequence not purpose); Maʿārif al-Qurʾān on 11:119 (both readings of "for that"). *Secondary (standard — verify identifier):* U. Rudolph, *Al-Māturīdī and the Development of Sunnī Theology in Samarqand* (Brill, 2015; German 1997) — khalq/fiʿl, khidhlān; J. Hoover, *Ibn Taymiyya's Theodicy of Perpetual Optimism* (Brill, 2007; open access) — the two wills, secondary causality, human agency; F. Griffel, *Al-Ghazālī's Philosophical Theology* (OUP, 2009) — al-Ghazālī's cosmology undecided between occasionalism and secondary causality; W. C. Chittick, *The Sufi Path of Knowledge* (SUNY, 1989) and Chittick's summaries of the Fuṣūṣ (Ibn ʿArabī Society) — sirr al-qadar, "knowledge follows the known", the ʿUzayr chapter; A. J. Freddoso, "God's General Concurrence with Secondary Causes: Why Conservation Is Not Enough," *Philosophical Perspectives* 5 (1991) 553–585, and "… Pitfalls and Prospects," *ACPQ* 67 (1994) 131–156 — the occasionalism / conservationism / concurrentism taxonomy; H. A. Wolfson, *The Philosophy of the Kalam* (Harvard, 1976); R. M. Frank, "The Structure of Created Causality according to al-Ašʿarī," *Studia Islamica* 25 (1966); D. Gimaret, *Théories de l'acte humain en théologie musulmane* (Vrin, 1980); W. M. Watt, *Free Will and Predestination in Early Islam* (Luzac, 1948); D. Perler and U. Rudolph, *Occasionalismus* (Vandenhoeck & Ruprecht, 2000); on al-Rāzī's "compelled in the form of a chooser" (muḍṭarr fī ṣūrat mukhtār), Rāzī's *al-Maṭālib al-ʿāliya* and *Tafsīr* as discussed in the recent Turkish literature (Danisname 4, 2022); on Ṣadrā's reading of amr bayn al-amrayn, the Asfār and the *Risāla fī khalq al-aʿmāl*, as discussed in *Journal of Islamic Theology and Philosophy* (Tehran, 2017); the hadith "lā jabr wa-lā tafwīḍ" in al-Kulaynī, *al-Kāfī*, Kitāb al-tawḥīd. On evil as privation: Ibn Sīnā, *al-Shifāʾ, al-Ilāhiyyāt* IX.6; Ṣadrā, *al-Asfār* (the discussion of al-sharr); Ibn Taymiyya as in Hoover ch. 5; the hadith "al-khayr kulluhu fī yadayk wa-l-sharr laysa ilayk" (Muslim, Ṣalāt al-musāfirīn, duʿāʾ al-istiftāḥ); *Lisān al-ʿArab* s.v. ẓ-l-m. *Formal check:* `v2.2/verify/q_kernel.py` (Z3, 29/29 after the mechanism checks 8a–8d), `q_kernel.log`, `q_kernel.smt2`; corpus counts `v2.2/verify/quran/counts.py` (nūr 40 occurrences, no plural; ẓulumāt 23, no singular). *Cited by status in §19 (P13):* "the angels were created from light, the jinn from smokeless fire, and Adam from what has been described to you" — a hadith of ʿĀʾisha in Ṣaḥīḥ Muslim (Kitāb al-Zuhd, no. 2996), not a verse; the jinn's origin is also Qur'anic (55:15; 15:27).

**22.6 Unrefereed manuscript — prime-spectral framework (clue 6b; 15 October 2026).** *The Prime-Spectral Framework: Theorems, Conjectures, and Numerical Tests* (LaTeX, user-supplied; repository cited as `MIKEAA2020/prime-numbers-in-physics`; same circle as §22.3). Status: unpublished and unrefereed. Theorem layer independently checked numerically — 35/35, `v2.2/verify/primon_checks.py` and `.log`; exploratory sector-ladder probe `v2.2/verify/sector_probe.py`; full review `v2.2/assessments/prime-spectral-review.md`. Not re-derived: the layer-graded moment, replica and dipole propositions of its §3 and the large-dimension Krylov/Chebyshev tiers; its Chebotarev protocol was assessed, not re-run. Used in §11.9 only. Clue 6a (the point–circle derivation of the Māturīdī mechanism) is assessed in §4.1 with checks 9a–9e; both clues are archived as `clues/clue-06a-maturidi.md` and `clues/clue-06b-prime-spectral.tex`.

**22.7 Chat transcript — the adelic image (clue 7; 15 October 2026).** A four-turn DeepSeek transcript of 4 October 2026 (`clues/clue-07-adelic-bridge-transcript.md`); the fourth turn is the "arithmetic crystal" image. Status: not a source of claims; sorted in §11.10 and `v2.2/assessments/adelic-bridge-review.md` with `v2.2/verify/adelic_checks.py` (12/12). Verified against primary sources: the Hartnoll–Yang abstract; Sierra 2014; BGGS 1992; Kleinschmidt–Koehn–Nicolai 2009. Not found: the three "unified frameworks" it names.

**22.8 Unrefereed preprint with reproducibility package — bounded sequential transduction (clue 8; 15 October 2026).** A. Abaee, Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.22260638 (v1, 2 Sep 2026; ≈89,700 words; 55 theorems). Status: a mathematics/CS monograph with no physics content; absorbed only into §10.6 (addendum), §4.2 (note) and N-1. A Bayesian brain perspective" (clue 12; Ciupa, LinkedIn, 8 Dec 2024; 15 October 2026; ninth amendment).** Text archived in `search/clue11/`; graded in `v2.2/assessments/clue-12-arrow-illusion-bayesian-brain-review.md`. **22.13 Gap audit — deeper web search over the open registers (15 October 2026; tenth amendment).** Full record: `v2.2/assessments/gap-audit-2026-10-15.md`; raw arXiv metadata in `search/gap1.xml`, `gap2.xml`, `gap3.xml`. *Method:* every open register read against the text; sixteen arXiv-API queries (2025–26) on the items with a live literature; twenty-eight abstracts read; journal fields from crossref. *Qur'anic side:* the plan had never cited the text's vocabulary for creature-time; the verses were verified against the local corpus (`verify/quran/`), the classical positions on 22:47/32:5/70:4 checked (Ṭabarī, Ibn Kathīr, Rāzī: the referent of the thousand and the fifty thousand is disputed; the relativity of the creatures' reckoning is not), and a tafsir-neutral entry written with an explicit guard against the time-dilation reading (§5 "Time, in the text"). *Physics side — three findings, entering three places* (count corrected 15 October 2026, eleventh amendment, item 106)*:* (i) closed-universe Hilbert spaces are one-dimensional without an observer and ≈ e^{S_Ob}-dimensional with one (Harlow–Usatyuk–Zhao, JHEP 2026; Abdalla–Antonini–Iliesiu–Levine, JHEP 2025; Akers et al., JHEP 2025) — the non-perturbative cousin of the plan's split (§9.3 item 2); (ii) clocks need not be added by hand — built from the fields (Speranza, CQG 2025) or from the dressing time (Freidel–Kirklin, JHEP 2026) (§11.4); (iii) York time inside a crossed product (Blommaert–Chen 2026, preprint) — the first construction in which D-14's two branches meet and Conjecture M's two senses of measure are one object, with the entropy's form becoming clock-dependent (§12.3/D-14; the T2′ caveat; P15). The third is the most consequential for the plan and is not an instance of the second: it changes a register entry and a caveat rather than adding a construction. *Status updates:* §7.1 (Oreshkov–Bouvy on stationarity, registered not adopted); §7.5 (the GIE interpretive dispute; no discriminating experiment); §9.3 items 1–3; §11.4; §12.3; §16.3; §17.1(a)–(b); §17.2; D-8, D-14, P2, P15, G2, G10, G12, G15, G17, G18, G19; WP0, WP1, WP4, WP6; dead end 8. *Searched, nothing new:* G9/WP5; D-11; D-10 beyond typicality; the arithmetic templates; clocks in superposition. *Verdict:* no Part I or Part II grade changed; no gap closed or opened; no dead end reopened. Status: audit complete; the plan's largest conceptual gap remains P10 (the dictionary from gradation to measurement), and its oldest unfinished verification WP3 step 2 (appendix proofs C.8–C.28).

**22.14 Adjudication A-1 — annealed replica transition of monitored circuits (15 October 2026; eleventh amendment; a separate lineage, not a clue).** A. Abaee, *manuscript_revised_v32* (revtex source; `adjudications/adj-01-a-manuscript_revised_v32.txt`), with an analyst/reviewer report contesting its n = 3 claim (`adjudications/adj-01-b-engine-contradicts-paper.txt`). Not a document about time and not numbered among the clues (clue 13 remains unassigned): it opens a separate lineage of *adjudications* (A-1, A-2, …) — disputes the user asks to have settled that have no bearing on the problem of time — kept in `/home/user/adjudications/` and reviewed in `assessments/adj-NN-*.md`. Examined because the user asked for the dispute to be settled and because the author is the author of clues 5 and 8. Full record: `v2.2/assessments/adj-01-replica-potts-dispute.md`; code `v2.2/verify/replica/` (independent transfer operator, Lanczos to L = 16, power iteration at L = 18, diagnostics); fast subset `verify/replica_checks.py`, 17/17 after the settlement run. *Exact layer:* every number the paper prints was reproduced — the two-replica closure, the Weingarten weights, the negative bond weights, the L = 6 spectrum with its sector assignment, the n = 2 control to L = 32, the n = 3 crossing table (p* to 3 × 10⁻⁴, ratios to 10⁻³). *Interpretive layer:* the claim that the amplitude ratio x_σ/x_ε "converges to 1/6" (3-state Potts) is contradicted by the paper's own sequence, which passes 1/6 near L = 11 and reaches 0.178 (L = 14) and 0.183 (L = 16) with increments decaying more slowly than L⁻²; the report's counter-claim R∞ = 0.194 ± 0.005 is a 1/L² extrapolation that other admissible forms put at 0.20–0.25. *What both missed:* the six-state model is exactly an Ising parity variable gating two 3-state Potts species (the class structure of S₃), and the parity sector (sgn ⊗ sgn) goes critical within Δp ≈ 0.004 of the σ sector (crossings 0.3090–0.3102 against 0.3026–0.3052), so every amplitude ratio at L ≤ 16 is a crossover quantity; the anisotropy-free check c/x_σ = 12A/X_σ gives ≈ 9 (3-state Potts: 6; n = 2 calibration recovers the Ising value 4 to 1 %). If the two transitions stay distinct, the σ transition is 3-state Potts by symmetry and the paper's class is right for a reason it never gave; if they merge — the separation of the two crossings falls as ≈ L^{−1.5} through L = 18 (power iteration on 10⁷ states), parity has no support of its own in the bare weights, and the large-d limit is a first-order 6-state transition — it is not, and the transition may be weakly first-order. Status at L ≤ 18: the paper's numerical argument fails; the report's extrapolation fails; the class is undecided. *Settlement run (same day; assessment §10; `verify/replica/replica_vumps.py`, `replica_n4.py`; `replica_checks.py` now 17/17).* The dominant eigenvector was computed directly in the thermodynamic limit as a uniform matrix-product state (VUMPS, χ = 24, 48, 72; the construction reproduces the dense operator to 10⁻¹⁷ and, on the n = 2 Ising point, gives κ = 2.05 against 2.03, β/ν = 0.127 against 0.125 and no hysteresis). Result: **one transition, not two** — the species and parity order parameters vanish together at p_c = 0.3049(2) at every χ, and no parity-ordered intermediate phase exists at any χ (finite χ would have enlarged one, not suppressed it); p = 0.3075 is in the disordered phase (parity order < 5 × 10⁻⁴, ξ_par = 9.8 at χ = 48). The order-parameter jumps at the transition (≈ 0.56 species, ≈ 0.39 parity) do not shrink from χ = 24 to 72, the latent-heat proxy Δ(df/dp) levels at ≈ 0.11, and the coexistence correlation lengths (≈ 35 at χ = 72) grow only as χ^{0.7}: most likely a **weakly first-order** transition with ξ ≈ 40–60, at the outside a continuous transition in a class with both order parameters critical — **not 3-state Potts in either case**, and every L ≤ 18 ratio (the paper's → 1/6, the report's 0.194) a crossover quantity at L < ξ. At n = 4 the paper's std ⊗ std crossings (0.3583, 0.3790) and ratio sequence (0.131, 0.183, 0.214) reproduce exactly, but the sgn, std ⊗ sgn and two-dimensional sectors cross at 0.370–0.394 (parity moving *away* from σ between (4,6) and (6,8), with ξ_par ≈ 3 lattice units at L ≤ 8): the "q = 4 Potts with logarithmic corrections" assignment is a single-sector statement and is keyed as unestablished, p_c⁽⁴⁾ ≈ 0.38 being the only robust number. Status: **settled against both sides' asymptotic claims; p_c⁽³⁾ = 0.3049(2) (paper's 0.305(3) confirmed); class: single transition, not 3-state Potts, weakly first-order most likely.** Re-keying recommendation in the assessment §7 and §10.3. Bearing on Parts I–II: none; the author profile of §22.3/§22.8 is confirmed a third time (exact layer verifies; interpretation overreaches).

**A-1 follow-up, 16 October 2026 (twelfth amendment; items 109–112).** The user assessed the improvement plan and asked for implementation. Archive: `adjudications/adj-01-c-v33-assessment.md`; revised assessment: `assessments/adj-01-paper-improvement-plan-v2.md`; implemented author-review source and diff: `papers/v33/`. The χ = 96 disordered point completed (p = 0.3050, f_b = 0.99350654, ξ_χ = 45.4, order diagnostics below 0.001); the approximate linear-branch crossing is 0.30487, preserving p_c ≈ 0.3049(2). The prior absolute wording “no intermediate phase exists” is superseded by “none found in the sampled scans.” Finite-χ lengths of 40–60 are not measured coexistence lengths; fixed-p effective slopes are not asymptotic exponent bounds. The headline Potts assignments are withdrawn, not replaced by a proved first-order class. The regular bimodule and n! conditional ordered-state orbit are standard algebra (already in v32 / Vasseur et al.); reduced symmetry and Schur–Weyl quotients prevent a universality theorem from the label count. The n* ∈ (2,3) scenario is conditional on first order at n = 3 and a regular connecting phase boundary, and the n → 1 fit is conjectural.

The Clifford 4^(−N_rand) SMC is implemented with an adapted proposal, exact tableau updates, finite-time anchors near and away from the transition, and population/genealogy diagnostics: 76 pilot/control/validation runs, including L = 128 and a population ladder to 16000. At p = 0.305 the L = 64/128 pilot has only 4–5 / 1–2 distinct mid-time ancestors despite appreciable incremental ESS, so **no coexistence verdict is obtained**. Sign-free and truncation-free do not mean bias-free or polynomial relative-error cost. The fourth Clifford commutant for the two-qubit gates has exact Gram rank **29**, not an independent 30-label stable-regime basis; its critical point and any Haar–Clifford critical-point split are uncomputed.

The paper's CTD/CPD and record results are independent of the class dispute, but not thereby globally re-proved. The revision retains their central statements and corrects separate scope errors: the optional CPD amplified-promise thresholds require n ≥ 3; bounded intensive variance does not justify replica/volume interchange (an explicit Bernoulli counterexample is supplied); the sufficient condition is made uniformly extensive in the tilted variance; no-freezing is restricted to appropriate analytic nondegenerate branches, with the p = 1 Haar analyticity hypothesis not claimed verified; Clifford efficient trajectories do not prove efficient estimation of all exponential tilts. The 35/35 extension checks validate this implementation, exact rank, counterexamples and document contracts. Together with a fresh 162/162 legacy run they give 197/197 across nine suites, run in two commands; the integrated driver now includes the extension. The v33 draft is not submission-ready: the original figure/SM are missing and author/specialist review is required. No statement of Parts I–II is altered.

**A-1 continuation, 16 October 2026 (thirteenth amendment; items 113–116).** The user requested a concrete next method, publication to `MIKEAA2020/arena-max--physics`, and dependency-ordered computation. The chosen primary method is deterministic transfer-MPS/DMRG on the exact six-label n=3 operator. Existing VUMPS is already in that family; it is not advertised as an independent alternative. A globally Hermitian half-row direct sum H+H† with paired environments reduces the MPO bond dimension from 36 to 12, with normalization f_b=−2 log eta_half. The naive nonsymmetric optimizer failed a complex-eigenvalue check and was rejected. Exact finite-ring identities, Ising scaling, period-Rayleigh cross-checks, checkpoint round trips and exact contraction reductions are verified. Half- and full-period finite-chi functionals are not identical, and local residuals are not global free-energy error certificates.

The actual campaign, including converged, unconverged, planned-stop and budget-stop states, is recorded in `research/transition-order/RESULTS.md`, `NUMERICAL-NOTES.md`, `optimizer-segments.json` and the raw JSON/logs. NPZ files contain real AL/AR/AC/C/environment tensors, not merely observations. Some disordered warm stages did not meet tolerance; their states may initialize a later solve but are not promoted to accepted phase observations. Higher-chi and same-p branch comparisons remain subject to their own convergence and feasibility gates. No new order theorem or measured latent heat is asserted.

The fourth Clifford local transfer is now derived on 30 closed labels with Gram rank 29 and a pseudoinverse quotient. Physical gate-generator checks, endpoints and Haar controls pass. All 52 requested Haar/Clifford L=4,6 cases in five diagonal colour sectors completed with small residuals; they resolve a finite-size difference, not a thermodynamic critical-point split. The L=8 Clifford matvec benchmark exceeded the adopted runtime gate and the larger ladder was deferred. Exact n=3,d=3 crossings and bidirectional small-chi pilots, and a low-chi n=4 Haar pilot with exact fully monitored control, were also executed. A dense n=4 full-period MPO and naive n=5 L=8 solve violate the resource policy; the higher-replica quotient/symmetry-reduction requirements are logged. Finite-cylinder interfaces/gaps, guided/resample-move SMC and full-Haar analytic hypotheses remain separate gated tasks. The previous SMC pilot's failure is not a no-go theorem for the entire method class. The regular-bimodule algebra is not conditional on n=3 being first order.

A fresh integrated regression passed 246/246 across ten suites (including 49 transfer-extension checks), exit 0. Original v32 and the historical v33 delivery report are preserved. Generated work and narrowly identified reproduction inputs were pushed with ordinary non-force commits, preserving the existing history/license; exact secret values and archive contents were scanned before staging. Credentials remain outside committed files, in excluded private Git metadata; that transient storage is not a durable vault. The initial nested corpus-copy inclusion is corrected in the current tree without rewriting history. Publication scope and the first verified commit are recorded in the repository. The draft still needs its original figure/SM, author review and proof-hypothesis audit before any submission certification. No Qur’anic reading, ontological commitment or statement of Parts I–II is altered; protected source hashes are stored.

## §24 Revision rules and provenance

**Rules [C].**

1. Identifiers are stable. An item is answered, split, merged, struck, or marked unanswerable — never silently removed. Struck items keep their number and appear in §21 with a reopening condition.
2. Every load-bearing physical claim carries one of the three registers; every framework statement carries one of the four grades. Promotion requires a citation or a proof.
3. Every change from a source is logged in §23 with its reason and the criterion that forced it.
4. Qur'anic citations appear only in the first-principles register. No verse is matched to a theory, result, or mechanism (§6, items 2, 3). The audit applies this rule to its own remarks.
5. No structure of physics or mathematics is identified with, pictured as, or said to image the ground (§1; §6, items 4, 17). The ground is not a term of any adjunction, fixed point, or algebra.
6. Statements about type II gravitational algebras carry "at leading semiclassical order" until G18 is closed.
7. Citations carry one of the three statuses of §22; "standard — verify identifier" entries are checked before any external use.
8. Where a source conflicts with v2.1, v2.1 governs; where v2.1 conflicts with the Lighthouse's first-principles register, the Lighthouse governs and v2.1 is amended with a note.
9. The D-7 forecast is scored annually against the dated register (RQ-8); WP milestones are reviewed at the stated years; failures are entered in §20.
10. A reader must be able to reconstruct every verdict in this document from §23 together with the criterion named there.
11. Readings of the Qur'an are established on textual grounds (§4.1, H0–H2) before they are used. No framework commitment — including the Lighthouse's governance under rule 8 and the user's override of the time document's non-dual assumption — is a premise of a reading; where a reading so established conflicts with a framework commitment, the reading governs and the framework is amended with a note.

**Provenance.** v2.2 was amended a thirteenth time on 16 October 2026 by the A-1 method-selection, feasibility-ordered numerical continuation and authorized GitHub publication (§22.14 continuation; items 113–116; 246/246 fresh integrated checks across ten suites). No part of the governing commitments or Parts I–II was changed. v2.2 was amended a twelfth time on 16 October 2026 by the A-1 v33 assessment and implementation (§22.14 follow-up; items 109–112; `papers/v33/`; 35/35 extension checks, 197/197 together with the legacy full run). Headline class claims were withdrawn, independent claims correctly scoped, χ = 96 completed and a tilted-Clifford pilot validated without a coexistence verdict; Parts I–II were unchanged. v2.2 was amended an eleventh time on 15 October 2026 by adjudication A-1 (a separate lineage, not a clue) — a monitored-circuits manuscript by the author of clues 5 and 8 against a report contesting its n = 3 universality claim (§22.14; `assessments/adj-01-replica-potts-dispute.md`; `verify/replica/`, `replica_checks.py` 13/13): both sides' numbers reproduced, both inferences rejected, the parity-gated Potts structure and the second critical sector found; and by a correction of the tenth amendment's headline (item 106). v2.2 was amended a tenth time on 15 October 2026 by a gap audit — a deeper web search over every open register (§22.13; `assessments/gap-audit-2026-10-15.md`): the Qur'an's own vocabulary for creature-time entered Part I as a lexical entry with a guard against the time-dilation reading of 22:47/70:4 (§5); the 2025–26 closed-universe observer results and the intrinsic-clock constructions — including the York-time crossed product where D-14's two branches meet — entered §9.3, §11.4, §12.3 and the registers; a dozen status notes were added; a verification runner and a status dashboard were added; §23.2 items 97–105. The physical and mathematical content is drawn from the published literature as cited; the three corrections to v2.0 (Proposition 10.1, Proposition 11.1, and the Connes–Størmer restatement), Conjecture M, and the verification script of §22.3 are the audit's own and should be checked by a specialist before being relied on. Nothing in this document has been peer-reviewed. The compiler is an agent on Arena.ai; the governing commitments are the user's, as stated in the Lighthouse.

**Closing statement.** The problem of time is not solved here, and no reconciliation of quantum mechanics with general relativity is claimed. What this document does is narrower: it fixes what a programme governed by the Qur'an, logic, and evidence may and may not say about time; it removes from the plan everything the non-dual premise had put there; it corrects three statements that were wrong as mathematics; it verifies the one piece of the plan's own mathematics that had been taken on report, and finds it true and smaller than reported; it brings the plan up to date with the one line of recent work that carries out its central move; and it leaves, in registers that cannot be quietly edited, the open questions P1–P15, fourteen predictions with falsifiers, twenty gaps, and eighteen dead ends. The ground is prior in being, not in time; time belongs to the created order; and how the created order comes to have it is a question for construction, which is what the work packages are for.
